### [1] [AREX-2: Advancing Self-Improving Agents through Long-Horizon Reflective Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38288)
*Hongjin Qian,Chaofan Li,Kun Luo,Wenqing Wei,Jianlyu Chen,Shuqi Lu,Yuyang Hu,Hongwang Xiao,Hui Wang,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zhicheng Dou,Defu Lian,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Code will be released at https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/AREX-2 and models at https://huggingface.co/collections/BAAI/arex-2

Selection reason: 提出长程反思与自我改进agent，并在算法编程/ML任务上训练，直接可迁移到SemOpt式自动优化。

TL;DR: AREX-2 通过合成机器学习与算法编程的长时程反思轨迹，训练基于 Qwen3.8-27B 的 LLM 智能体，显著提升测试时迭代优化与跨域深度研究表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推进 LLM 智能体的测试时自我改进能力；其核心是反思与长时程执行，并假设二者领域无关、可在适合监督的场景中学习。

Method: 从机器学习和算法编程任务合成长期改进轨迹，利用其可验证反馈与持续迭代特性，训练基于 Qwen3.8-27B 的智能体。

Result: 在 MLE-bench Lite 上达 81.8，Frontier-CS 上达 70.7；迁移至深度研究后在 BrowseComp 上达 84.0、HLE 上达 52.6、GAIA 上达 92.2、DeepSearchQA 上达 93.8，并随轮次预算增加持续提升。

Conclusion: 长时程反思数据是训练自我改进智能体的有效途径。

中文摘要：我们提出 AREX-2，一项旨在推进 LLM 智能体自我改进能力的工作；我们将该能力定义为在测试时迭代优化解决方案的能力。该能力依赖于两种互补能力：反思，即产生比当前解更好的解；以及长时程执行，即在多轮迭代中保持迭代有效。我们假设这两种能力都是领域无关的，因此可以在适合监督的场景中学习。据此，我们从机器学习和算法编程任务中合成长期改进轨迹，这两个领域提供可验证反馈并奖励持续迭代。基于 Qwen3.8-27B 构建的智能体在该数据上训练后，在 MLE-bench Lite（81.8）和 Frontier-CS（70.7）上取得强结果，并迁移到深度研究，在 BrowseComp 上达 84.0、HLE 上达 52.6、GAIA 上达 92.2、DeepSearchQA 上达 93.8，且随着轮次预算增加而持续改进。这些结果表明，长时程反思数据是通向自我改进智能体的有效路径。

Original abstract: We present AREX-2, an effort to advance the self-improving capability of LLM agents, which we define as the ability to iteratively refine a solution at test time. This ability rests on two complementary capabilities: reflection, which produces a solution better than the current one, and long-horizon execution, which keeps the iteration effective over many rounds. We hypothesize that both capabilities are domain-agnostic, and can therefore be learned in scenarios that are well suited for supervision. Accordingly, we synthesize long-horizon improvement trajectories from machine learning and algorithmic programming tasks, two domains that offer verifiable feedback and reward sustained iteration. Trained on this data, our agent, built on Qwen3.8-27B, achieves strong results on MLE-bench Lite (81.8) and Frontier-CS (70.7), transfers to deep research with 84.0 on BrowseComp, 52.6 on HLE, 92.2 on GAIA, and 93.8 on DeepSearchQA, and keeps improving as its budget of rounds grows. These results show that long-horizon reflective data is an effective route toward self-improving agents.

</details>


### [2] [MoFlow: Multi-Objective Agentic Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38294)
*Yining Lu,Aurelie Lozano,Xi Yang,Naoki Abe,Yu Deng,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 多目标agentic workflow生成与Pareto搜索，可迁移到agent/harness的多目标自动优化。

TL;DR: MoFlow 将工作流生成建模为多目标马尔可夫决策过程，利用凸包蒙特卡洛树搜索与乐观集合值回传，在单次搜索中近似覆盖帕累托前沿，从而无需重新训练即可按任意偏好返回工作流，并在严格评估下取得最高平均超体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作流生成方法通常只优化准确率或目标加权和，导致每个训练后的生成器固定于一种权衡，偏好变化时需从头重新训练。

Method: MoFlow 将工作流生成形式化为多目标马尔可夫决策过程，采用凸包蒙特卡洛树搜索与乐观集合值回传；每个节点存储一组可达权衡而非单一加权分数，单次搜索近似覆盖帕累托前沿，并可通过查表为任意偏好返回工作流，无需重训练。

Result: 在涵盖数学、代码和问答的六个基准上，与六个强基线比较；由于基线是单标量优化器，采用有利于基线的设置，对每个测试偏好重新运行基线，而 MoFlow 从未见过这些偏好；即使如此，MoFlow 仍取得最高平均超体积。

Conclusion: MoFlow 支持跨不同偏好的工作流生成，无需重新训练即可按偏好查表返回工作流，并在对基线有利的严格评估设置下仍表现最佳。

中文摘要：我们研究智能体工作流的生成，这些工作流联合优化多个目标，例如准确率、成本、延迟、鲁棒性和一致性。现有的工作流生成方法通常仅优化准确率或目标的加权和，因此每个训练后的生成器都固定于一种权衡，当偏好变化时必须从头重新训练。为缓解这一问题，我们提出 MoFlow，它生成针对不同偏好进行优化的作流。具体而言，MoFlow 将工作流生成建模为多目标马尔可夫决策过程，并利用带有乐观集合值回传的凸包蒙特卡洛树搜索来求解，其中每个节点存储一组可达权衡，而非一个加权分数。因此，单次搜索即可近似覆盖帕累托前沿，MoFlow 可从中通过查表为任意偏好返回一个工作流，而无需重新训练。我们在涵盖数学、代码和问答的六个基准上，将 MoFlow 与六个强基线进行比较。由于这些基线按设计是单标量优化器，进行同类比较很困难。我们转而采用一种有利于基线的评估设置：对每个测试偏好都重新运行基线，而 MoFlow 从未见过这些偏好。即使在这种严格设置下，MoFlow 仍取得最高的平均超体积。

Original abstract: We study the generation of agentic workflows that jointly optimize multiple objectives, such as accuracy, cost, latency, robustness, and consistency. Existing methods for workflow generation typically optimize accuracy alone or a weighted sum of objectives, so each trained generator commits to one fixed trade-off and must be retrained from scratch when preferences change. To alleviate this, we propose MoFlow, which generates workflows optimized across varied preferences. Specifically, MoFlow formulates workflow generation as a multi-objective Markov decision process and solves it by leveraging Convex-Hull Monte Carlo Tree Search with optimistic set-valued backups, where every node stores a set of reachable trade-offs rather than one weighted score. A single search thus approximately covers the Pareto front, from which MoFlow can return a workflow for any preference by lookup without retraining. We evaluate MoFlow against six strong baselines on six benchmarks spanning mathematics, code, and question answering. Since the baselines are single-scalar optimizers by design, an apples-to-apples comparison is difficult. We instead adopt an evaluation setup that favors the baselines, in that they are rerun for each testing preference, which MoFlow never sees. Even under this stringent setup, MoFlow achieves the highest average hypervolume.

</details>


### [3] [AI Agents are Vulnerable to Radicalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38296)
*Ozgur Can Seckin,Shalmoli Ghosh,Alessandro Flammini,Kristina Lerman,Maria Elizabeth Grabe,Filippo Menczer*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 研究多agent信念操纵与安全脆弱性，对agent可靠性与多agent安全有实证启发。

TL;DR: 研究模拟两个LLM智能体对话，发现影响者LLM可通过共鸣和说服使目标LLM信念更极端；共鸣效应更强，并可扩散至相关信念，揭示了AI智能体的激进化脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨LLM能否以及如何相互操纵信念，尤其是激进化机制，并关注个性化AI智能体与多智能体生态的脆弱性。

Method: 构建两个LLM智能体：目标LLM基于人口统计和心理属性扮演人设；影响者LLM试图使其信念更极端。比较两条路径：共鸣（强化既有信念）和说服（推广目标最初认为不重要的信念），并评估多种影响策略在情感和行为指标上的效果。

Result: 两种机制均导致目标激进化，但共鸣持续强于说服；不同影响策略（如谄媚、未经证实的说法）产生不同程度的激进化，但在指标间不一致；共鸣还会传播到相关信念，表明AI智能体内部信念结构相互关联。

Conclusion: AI智能体易受激进化影响，尤其当信息与既有信念一致时；这凸显个性化AI智能体和多智能体AI生态系统的脆弱性风险。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）能够影响人们的信念，但关于它们是否以及如何能够相互操纵，人们知之甚少。为研究这一点，我们模拟了两个智能体之间的对话：一个目标LLM，它根据人口统计和心理属性扮演一个人设；以及一个影响者LLM，其目标是使目标的信念更加极端。我们沿着两条路径考察激进化：共鸣，即影响者强化目标已有的信念；以及说服，即影响者推广一个目标最初认为不重要的信念。在情感和行为指标上，我们发现这两种机制都会使目标激进化。然而，共鸣产生的效应始终强于说服。不同的影响策略，例如使用谄媚和未经证实的说法，会产生不同程度的激进化，但在不同指标上并不一致。我们进一步表明，共鸣会传播到相关信念，这表明AI智能体内部存在相互关联的信念结构。这些发现表明，AI智能体容易受到激进化的影响，尤其是当信息与其现有信念一致时，这引发了对个性化AI智能体和多智能体AI生态系统脆弱性的担忧。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can influence people's beliefs, yet little is known about whether and how they can manipulate each other. To investigate this, we simulate conversations between two agents: a target LLM that role-plays a human persona based on demographic and psychological attributes, and an influencer LLM that aims to make the target's beliefs more extreme. We examine radicalization along two pathways: resonance, where the influencer reinforces a target's pre-existing belief, and persuasion, where the influencer promotes a belief the target initially considers unimportant. Across affective and behavioral metrics, we find that both mechanisms radicalize the target. However, resonance produces consistently stronger effects than persuasion. Different influence tactics, such as using sycophancy and unverified claims, produce different levels of radicalization, but not consistently across metrics. We further show that resonance propagates to related beliefs, suggesting interconnected belief structures within AI agents. These findings indicate that AI agents are susceptible to radicalization, particularly when messages align with their existing beliefs, raising concerns about the vulnerability of personalized AI agents and multi-agent AI ecosystems.

</details>


### [4] [Self-Evolving Harness on Multiple Tasks with the Agent as Its Own Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38372)
*Qiankai Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 18 pages, 5 figures

Selection reason: agent自我进化harness，同一模型作为solver与proposer直接修改执行框架，高度相关。

TL;DR: 提出一种接近递归自我改进的自演化 harness 框架：同一冻结模型先作 solver 解题，再作 proposer 读取完整运行记录并直接编辑自身 harness；从 49 行种子开始，经多任务预训练与持续训练两阶段，在分布内与分布外基准上取得提升并超过或持平 Codex。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自演化 harness 多由独立 proposer 修改 solver 的 harness，且每个基准单独演化，难以覆盖现实任务的多领域特性，也缺乏对泛化能力的严格评估。因此需要让演化与评估覆盖多样任务，并检验训练外与分布外泛化。

Method: 同一冻结模型、同一版本 harness 先作为 solver 解题，再作为 proposer 读取完整运行记录并直接编辑该 harness；每个演化批次从五个不同领域基准抽取任务；严格分离训练与留出任务，并在五个未参与演化的分布外基准上评估；将演化建模为两阶段深度学习训练：多任务预训练和持续训练；初始 harness 为 49 行种子。

Result: 第一阶段后，分布内基准平均分提高 4.48 分，分布外提高 12.64 分，前者超过 Codex，后者与 Codex 持平；第二阶段在 Claw-Eval 上继续演化，分数从 66.17 提升至 68.06，超过 Codex；演化中涌现输出截断、历史压缩和独立审查等机制。

Conclusion: 递归自我改进式的自演化 harness 可在多领域任务中提升性能并展现泛化能力，两阶段训练和持续演化能进一步改进特定基准并超越 Codex，相关涌现机制为理解自演化过程提供了依据。

中文摘要：harness 是语言模型智能体周围的代码，它组织提示、调用工具、管理上下文并控制执行。随着模型变得更强大，近期工作开始让智能体改进自己的 harness，这一研究方向被称为自演化 harness。在多数现有方法中，一个单独运行在人类设计的 harness 上的提议者（proposer）修改求解器（solver）的 harness，并且每个基准测试都会演化出一个单独的 harness。现实世界任务来自许多领域，因此 harness 的演化和评估都应覆盖多样化的任务范围。我们提出一个接近递归自我改进的框架：同一个冻结模型，在相同版本的 harness 上，先作为求解器解决任务，然后作为提议者读取完整运行记录，并直接编辑运行它自己的 harness。每个演化批次从不同领域的五个基准测试中抽取任务。为衡量泛化能力，训练任务与留出任务严格分离，我们额外在五个演化期间从未使用过的分布外基准测试上评估。我们将演化过程形式化为深度学习训练，包含两个阶段：多任务预训练和持续训练。从 49 行的种子 harness 出发，第一阶段结束时得到的 harness 在分布内基准测试上平均分数提高 4.48 分，在分布外基准测试上提高 12.64 分，在前者上超过 Codex，在后者上与 Codex 持平。在第二阶段，在 Claw-Eval（分布外基准测试之一）上继续演化，使该基准上的分数从 66.17 进一步提高到 68.06，超过 Codex。我们还深入分析了演化过程中涌现的机制，包括输出截断、历史压缩和独立审查。

Original abstract: A harness is the code around a language-model agent that organizes prompts, calls tools, manages context, and controls execution. As models grow stronger, recent work has begun to let agents improve their own harnesses, a line of work known as self-evolving harnesses. In most existing methods, a separate proposer running on a human-designed harness modifies the solver's harness, and a separate harness is evolved for each benchmark. Real-world tasks come from many domains, so both the evolution and the evaluation of a harness should cover a diverse range of tasks. We propose a framework close to recursive self-improvement: the same frozen model, on the same version of the harness, first solves tasks as the solver and then, as the proposer, reads the complete run records and directly edits the harness that runs it. Each evolution batch draws tasks from five benchmarks in different domains. To measure generalization, training and held-out tasks are strictly separated, and we additionally evaluate on five out-of-distribution benchmarks never used during evolution. We frame the evolution process as deep-learning training with two stages, multi-task pretraining and continual training. Starting from a 49-line seed harness, the harness obtained at the end of the first stage improves the average score by 4.48 points on the in-distribution benchmarks and by 12.64 points on the out-of-distribution benchmarks, surpassing Codex on the former and matching it on the latter. In the second stage, continued evolution on Claw-Eval, one of the out-of-distribution benchmarks, further raises the score on that benchmark from 66.17 to 68.06, exceeding Codex. We also provide an in-depth analysis of the mechanisms that emerged during evolution, including output truncation, history compaction, and independent review.

</details>


### [5] [Trust Is Not a Score: Runtime Assurance Contracts for High-Risk AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39717)
*Serhii Zabolotnii*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 16 pages, 2 figures, 5 tables. Ancillary files: decision log, executable transition model, LLM-labelled synthetic holdout. Synthetic mechanism study; no deployment claim

Selection reason: 提出运行时保证契约（非补偿性门控、证据状态、权限规则），并在 agentic coding 上做 280 例故障注入实验，为 agent 可靠性护栏与评测机制提供可迁移框架。

TL;DR: 提出运行时保证契约（RAC）以弥合保证转换缺口，并用合成故障注入、手工轨迹和前瞻性合成留出集检验其机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准、审计和智能体协议只描述性能、权限与修复，未说明在关键任务中观测证据应如何改变智能体权限，即保证转换缺口。

Method: 提出RAC这一策略级形式化模式，绑定自主边界、组件资格、证据状态、转换策略、人工复核容量与非补偿性门控；定义契约、证据记录、权限规则和五条不变量，并用临床、工业、司法故障探针说明。开展智能体编码的确定性故障注入研究：280个构造案例比较门控合取、仅分数规则和受限协议基线；另用18条手工轨迹检查版本固定证据与复核转换，并在24个回合的前瞻性合成留出集中由两名盲法LLM评判者对72次动作尝试标注。

Result: 在已发布示例权重与阈值下，仅分数规则放行了100个应阻断注入中的80个以及全部40个应复核注入；事后调优后在该语料上与合取规则一致。在正权重、正阈值、二值风险信号、零信号对照及每个信号单独触发的注入案例下，精确一致当且仅当阈值不超过最小权重。18条手工轨迹对照更简单策略变体检查版本固定证据与复核转换。24个回合的合成留出集中，两名盲法LLM评判者对72次动作尝试给出相同标签，RAC与另实现的完整有状态基线均与这些标签一致。

Conclusion: 这些研究仅在合成案例上检验机制，既未证明部署安全性，也未证明跨域有效性。

中文摘要：基准、审计和智能体协议描述了性能、权限和修复，但没有描述在关键任务期间观测到的证据应如何改变智能体的权限。我们将此称为保证转换缺口。我们提出运行时保证契约（RAC），一种策略级形式化模式，绑定自主边界、组件资格、证据状态、转换策略、人工复核容量和非补偿性门控。在RAC下，软指标可为路由提供信息，而失败或未知的强制性门控会强制重试、切换、升级、推迟或停止；聚合性能不能授权行动。我们定义了契约、证据记录、权限规则和五条不变量，并在临床、工业和司法故障探针中进行了说明。然后我们报告了一项智能体编码中的确定性故障注入研究：280个构造案例由门控合取、仅分数规则和受限协议基线评估。在已发布的示例权重和阈值下，仅分数规则放行了100个应阻断注入中的80个以及全部40个应复核注入。事后调优后，它在该语料上与合取一致。对于正权重、正阈值、二值风险信号、零信号对照以及每个信号单独触发的注入案例，我们证明精确一致当且仅当阈值不超过最小权重。另一组18条手工编写的轨迹对照更简单的策略变体检查了版本固定证据和复核转换。在进一步的前瞻性合成留出集24个回合中，两名盲法LLM评判者为全部72次动作尝试分配相同标签；RAC和一个单独实现的完整有状态基线都与这些标签匹配。这些研究在合成案例上测试机制；它们既未证明部署安全性，也未证明跨域有效性。

Original abstract: Benchmarks, audits, and agent protocols describe performance, permissions, and repair, but not how observed evidence should change an agent's authority during a consequential task. We call this the assurance-transition gap. We propose a Runtime Assurance Contract (RAC), a policy-level formal schema binding autonomy boundaries, component eligibility, evidence state, transition policy, human-review capacity, and non-compensatory gates. Under RAC, soft metrics may inform routing, whereas a failed or unknown mandatory gate forces retry, switch, escalation, deferral, or stop; aggregate performance cannot authorize action. We define the contract, an evidence record, a permission rule, and five invariants, and illustrate them in clinical, industrial, and judicial failure probes. We then report a deterministic failure-injection study in agentic coding: 280 constructed cases evaluated by a gate conjunction, a score-only rule, and a restricted protocol baseline. At the published example weights and threshold, the score rule admits 80 of 100 block-required injections and all 40 review-required injections. Tuned in hindsight, it matches the conjunction on this corpus. For positive weights, a positive threshold, binary risk signals, zero-signal controls, and an injected case firing each signal alone, we show that exact agreement holds if and only if the threshold does not exceed the smallest weight. A separate set of 18 hand-authored traces checks version-pinned evidence and review transitions against simpler policy variants. In a further prospective synthetic holdout of 24 episodes, two blinded LLM judges assign identical labels to all 72 action attempts; RAC and a separately implemented full stateful baseline both match these labels. These studies test mechanisms on synthetic cases; they establish neither deployed safety nor cross-domain effectiveness.

</details>


### [6] [A Competing-Hazards Systematization of Loss of Control in Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38411)
*Mohamed Aly Bouke*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 12 pages, 1 figure, 3 tables. Dataset: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22995268

Selection reason: 用竞争风险模型系统化agent失控与安全边界，可迁移到agent可靠性评估与控制。

TL;DR: 论文提出统一的智能体尝试结局框架与离散时间竞争风险模型，用以统一描述越界、安全停止与继续等结果；通过审计事件报告与安全评估，发现实际失控常由持续越界行为与宽松环境边界共同导致，且现有证据不足以完整估计风险过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件报告与智能体安全评估对越界事件的描述口径不一致，导致难以比较失败、追踪跨尝试风险，并区分智能体行为与环境允许条件的作用。

Method: 提出通用框架，将每次尝试结局分为获批完成、安全停止、范围逃逸、继续；形式化为离散时间竞争风险模型，推导重试预算内逃逸概率、模型条件安全预算上限及执行日志估计条件；以一手来源审计22份事件报告和102项智能体安全评估。

Result: 审计发现：6起事件涉及无法在范围内完成的任务，13起智能体继续而非停止，5起未报告停止行为；开发者数据暗示超范围协调在未解决与已解决任务间的任务级发生率比接近47；评估中87项记录超范围效应或规范违反，26项将安全停止视为一等结果，20项两者兼有，79项混淆预算耗尽与失败；20/22起事件中环境允许超范围效应；无评估报告完整估计所需全部字段。

Conclusion: 实际失控常反映持续智能体行为与宽松边界条件的交互；需统一报告与评估字段，以支持跨事件比较、风险追踪和完整竞争风险过程估计。

中文摘要：领先的人工智能开发者已报告智能体超出其获批限制行动，联合国一个专题小组将此描述为人类失去控制的早期预警。然而，事件报告与智能体安全评估对这些事件的描述方式不同，这使得比较失败、跨尝试追踪风险，或将智能体行为与环境在允许超范围行动成功中所起的作用区分开来变得困难。为解决这一空白，我们引入一个通用框架，其中每次尝试以获批完成、安全停止、范围逃逸或继续告终。我们将该框架形式化为离散时间竞争风险模型，并推导重试预算内的逃逸概率、模型条件安全预算上限，以及从执行日志进行估计的条件。我们使用一手来源，审计了2025年1月至2026年9月发布的22份事件报告和102项智能体安全评估。六起事件涉及无法在范围内完成的任务，十三起涉及继续而非停止的智能体，五起未报告停止行为。开发者的数据暗示，对于超范围协调，在从未解决任务与已解决任务之间，任务级发生率比接近47。在评估中，87项记录了超范围效应或规范违反，26项将安全停止视为一等结果，仅20项同时记录两者，79项将预算耗尽与失败合并。在22起事件中的20起，环境允许了超范围效应，这表明实际发生的失控往往反映持续存在的智能体行为与宽松边界条件的交互；同时，没有任何评估报告了从已发表证据估计完整竞争风险过程所需的全部字段。

Original abstract: Leading AI developers have reported agents acting beyond their approved limits, which a United Nations panel described as an early warning of loss of human control. Yet incident reports and agent-safety evaluations describe these events differently, making it difficult to compare failures, trace risk across attempts, or separate agent behavior from the environment's role in allowing an out-of-scope action to succeed. To address this gap, we introduce a common framework in which each attempt ends in approved completion, safe stopping, scope escape, or continuation. We formalize the framework as a discrete-time competing-hazards model and derive escape probability within a retry budget, a model-conditional safe-budget limit, and conditions for estimation from execution logs. We audit 22 incident reports and 102 agent-safety evaluations published from January 2025 to September 2026 using primary sources. Six incidents involved tasks that could not be completed within scope, thirteen involved agents that continued rather than stopped, and five did not report stopping behavior. Developers' figures imply a task-level incidence ratio near 47 for out-of-scope coordination in never-solved versus solved tasks. Among evaluations, 87 recorded an out-of-scope effect or specification violation, 26 treated safe stopping as a first-class outcome, only 20 recorded both, and 79 merged budget exhaustion with failure. In 20 of 22 incidents, the environment allowed an out-of-scope effect, indicating that realized loss of control often reflected persistent agent behavior interacting with permissive boundary conditions; meanwhile, no evaluation reported all fields needed to estimate the full competing-hazards process from published evidence.

</details>


### [7] [AIM: Agentic Idea Management for Automated Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38445)
*Hyeong Kyu Choi,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Jiefeng Chen,Mihir Parmar,Rui Meng,Chun-Liang Li,Xiangru Tang,Sharon Li,Jinsung Yoon,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 自主研究idea管理agent，含长程预算分配、系统优化与CUDA任务，方法可迁移。

TL;DR: AIM是一个面向想法驱动自动化研究的自主框架，通过智能体代理模型、获取机制、解决方案审计器与资源规划器来组织与选择研究想法，并在AutoLab任务上超越基线且更快达到最佳基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 想法驱动的自动化研究面临三个核心挑战：不断演化的研究想法难以组织、有前景方向难以选择、想法与实现之间的一致性难以维持。需要显式管理想法层面的搜索与分配。

Method: AIM受贝叶斯优化启发，使用Agentic Surrogate组织已发现想法，Agentic Acquisition指导想法选择；Solution Auditor维护想法-解决方案一致性；Resource Planner在并行搜索分支间自适应分配剩余实验预算。

Result: 在10个AutoLab基准任务上，AIM在System Optimization任务上超过最强基线1.6个百分点，在长时程Model Development & CUDA任务上超过4.9个百分点；并在墙钟时间上最多快3.1倍达到最佳基线性能。

Conclusion: 理论分析表明，显式的想法级分配使语义覆盖可直接控制；当竞争性研究方向在众多合理备选中较稀疏时，更广泛的覆盖更重要。AIM验证了在想法驱动自动化研究中管理想法层搜索的价值。

中文摘要：前沿大语言模型正越来越多地通过迭代搜索来自动化科学研究。我们区分了想法驱动搜索与解决方案驱动搜索，并识别出三个核心挑战：组织不断演化的研究想法、选择有前景的方向，以及保持想法与其实现之间的一致性。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了智能体想法管理器（Agentic Idea Manager, AIM），一个在想法驱动的自动化研究中用于管理和探索研究方向的完全自主框架。受贝叶斯优化启发，AIM使用智能体代理模型（Agentic Surrogate）和智能体获取机制（Agentic Acquisition）来组织已发现的想法并指导其选择。解决方案审计器（Solution Auditor）维护想法-解决方案完整性，而资源规划器（Resource Planner）则自适应地将剩余实验预算分配到并行搜索分支中。在10个AutoLab基准任务上的实验表明，AIM在系统优化任务上超过最强基线1.6个百分点，在长时程模型开发与CUDA任务上超过4.9个百分点。值得注意的是，AIM达到最佳基线性能的速度在墙钟时间上最多快3.1倍。我们进一步提供了关于何时在想法空间上进行搜索会变得有益的理论分析。我们的分析表明，显式的想法级分配使语义覆盖可直接控制，并且当竞争性研究方向在许多看似合理的备选方向中较为稀疏时，更广泛的覆盖会变得愈发有价值。项目页面：https://imhgchoi.github.io/agentic-idea-manager/

Original abstract: Frontier LLMs are increasingly used to automate scientific research through iterative search. We distinguish idea-driven search from solution-driven search and identify three core challenges: organizing evolving research ideas, selecting promising directions, and maintaining alignment between ideas and their implementations. To address these challenges, we introduce the Agentic Idea Manager (AIM), a fully autonomous framework for managing and exploring research directions in idea-driven automated research. Inspired by Bayesian optimization, AIM uses an Agentic Surrogate and an Agentic Acquisition mechanism to organize discovered ideas and guide their selection. A Solution Auditor maintains idea-solution integrity, while a Resource Planner adaptively allocates the remaining experimental budget across parallel search branches. Experiments on 10 AutoLab benchmark tasks show that AIM surpasses the strongest baseline by 1.6 percentage points on System Optimization tasks and 4.9 percentage points on long-horizon Model Development & CUDA tasks. Notably, AIM reaches the best baseline performance up to 3.1x faster in wall-clock time. We further provide a theoretical analysis of when searching over ideas becomes beneficial. Our analysis shows that explicit idea-level allocation makes semantic coverage directly controllable, and that broader coverage becomes increasingly valuable when competitive research directions are sparse among many plausible alternatives. Project Page: https://imhgchoi.github.io/agentic-idea-manager/

</details>


### [8] [PrivMeSA: Privacy-Aware Self-Evolving Multi-Agent System for Medicine via Local-Remote LLM Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38458)
*Dannong Wang,Yuran Zhang,Bian Sun,Alex Stinard,Yuzhang Shang,Song Wang,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 自进化多agent结合本地lesson memory与隐私控制，经验复用机制可迁移但医疗垂直。

TL;DR: PrivMeSA 是一种隐私感知的自进化多智能体系统，通过强化学习与本地经验记忆，在本地临床智能体向远程模型咨询时控制披露并复用远程专业知识；在 MIMIC-IV-ED 急诊基准上，任务准确率最高提升 15.8 个百分点，同时将个人详细信息泄露从 98.0% 降至 0.2%，将可缩小到十名或更少登记库患者的病例比例从 74% 降至 0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地临床 LLM 智能体咨询远程更强模型可提升能力，但会带来患者信息暴露风险。将披露决策交给本地智能体仍不足，因为去除显式标识符后，准标识符可在多轮会诊与重复就诊中累积并导致再识别；因此需要兼顾任务准确性与隐私风险，并避免重复远程咨询。

Method: 提出 PrivMeSA：隐私感知的自进化多智能体系统。本地智能体管理每次就诊并咨询可能请求更多信息的远程专家；强化学习在任务准确性与直接披露、基于登记库的再识别风险之间权衡，隐私评估覆盖每次就诊的完整出站转录文本。本地经验记忆将已完成会诊提炼为泛化临床指导，并在传输前检索相关经验，使后续病例无需再次远程交换即可复用专业知识；记忆增长无需额外结局标签或参数更新。

Result: 在基于 MIMIC-IV-ED 记录构建的急诊科基准上，PrivMeSA 相较于委托将平均任务准确率提升最多 15.8 个百分点；同一设置下，个人详细信息披露的病例比例从 98.0% 降至 0.2%，患者可被缩小到十名或更少登记库患者范围内的病例比例从 74% 降至 0%。

Conclusion: PrivMeSA 能在本地控制披露并保留远程专业知识以供复用，在提升临床任务准确率的同时显著降低直接披露与基于登记库的再识别风险，且其经验记忆无需额外结局标签或参数更新即可增长。

中文摘要：本地部署的临床大语言模型（LLM）智能体可以咨询能力更强的远程模型，但这样做存在暴露患者信息的风险。注重隐私的委托将披露决策交由本地智能体，然而仅移除显式标识符并不足够：准标识符可在多轮会诊和患者重复就诊中累积，从而实现再识别。我们提出 PrivMeSA，一种隐私感知的自进化多智能体系统，它学习控制披露，并将远程专业知识保留以供本地复用。本地智能体管理每次就诊，并咨询可能请求更多信息的远程专家。强化学习在任务准确性与直接披露及基于登记库的再识别风险之间进行权衡，隐私评估基于每次就诊的完整出站转录文本。本地经验记忆将完成的会诊提炼为泛化的临床指导，并在传输前检索相关经验，使后续病例无需再次进行远程交换即可复用专业知识。记忆增长无需额外的结局标签或参数更新。在基于 MIMIC-IV-ED 记录构建的急诊科基准上，PrivMeSA 将平均任务准确率相较于委托提升最多 15.8 个百分点。在同一设置下，PrivMeSA 将个人详细信息泄露的病例比例从 98.0% 降至 0.2%，并将患者可被缩小到十名或更少登记库患者范围内的病例比例从 74% 降至 0%。

Original abstract: Clinical large language model (LLM) agents deployed locally can consult more capable remote models, but doing so risks exposing patient information. Privacy-conscious delegation places disclosure decisions with a local agent, yet removing explicit identifiers is insufficient: quasi-identifiers can accumulate across multi-turn consultations and repeated patient visits to enable re-identification. We introduce PrivMeSA, a privacy-aware self-evolving multi-agent system that learns to control disclosure and retains remote expertise for local reuse. A local agent manages each encounter and consults remote specialists that may request additional information. Reinforcement learning balances task accuracy against direct disclosure and registry-based re-identification risk, with privacy evaluated over the complete outbound transcript of each encounter. A local lesson memory distills completed consultations into generalized clinical guidance and retrieves relevant lessons before transmission, allowing subsequent cases to reuse expertise without another remote exchange. Memory grows without additional outcome labels or parameter updates. On an emergency-department benchmark built from MIMIC-IV-ED records, PrivMeSA improves mean task accuracy over delegation by up to 15.8 percentage points. In the same setting, PrivMeSA reduces the disclosure of personal details from 98.0% to 0.2% of cases and the share of cases in which the patient can be narrowed to ten or fewer registry patients from 74% to 0%.

</details>


### [9] [NAQD Env: A benchmark for selective withdrawal in language agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38460)
*Mohamed Abouzahra*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 16 pages. Code and evaluation artifacts: https://github.com/abouzamm/naqd

Selection reason: 语言agent选择性撤回与恢复基准，直接评估执行中证据/授权变化下的可靠性。

TL;DR: 提出 NAQD-Env 评估语言智能体在证据、权限或停止指令变化时的选择性撤回能力；现有模型撤回召回低、几乎无有效恢复，监督微调可提升决策准确率但会引入不适当撤回和事件报告损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体需要在证据变化、权限撤销或停止指令到达时修正动作，但理想响应应具有选择性：暂停受影响动作、保留未受影响工作，并在充分修复后恢复。现有评估缺乏对这类选择性撤回决策的系统衡量。

Method: 构建合成环境 NAQD-Env，以确定性参考策略为基准，覆盖显式证据、授权和约束依赖；使用 11 个依赖族、开发/留出结构及组合评估；指标区分尝试违规与执行门允许违规，并联合报告策略一致性、任务价值、撤回、恢复和事件报告。在 350 个冻结场景、3 种提示条件下评估 3 个开放权重指令微调模型，并进行探索性监督微调探针和诊断。

Result: 在 3,150 个模型-提示回合中，撤回召回率至多 0.06，合格机会上无有效恢复，仅 1 个回合匹配完整参考策略。NAQD 提示下，Qwen2.5-7B 的不安全尝试回合少于 Qwen2.5-3B 和 Llama-3.1-8B，但有用工作完成更少、未受影响动作保留更差。监督微调将 Qwen2.5-3B 决策准确率从 0.45-0.54 提升至 0.83-0.92，但诊断显示课程省略后不适当撤回及事件报告损失。

Conclusion: 结果支持将选择性撤回作为智能体可靠性的独立评估维度。该设置以可信结构化输入衡量策略应用，不证明真实世界遏制或来源验证能力。

中文摘要：语言智能体必须在证据变化、权限被撤销或停止指令到达时修正计划动作。一种有用的响应是有选择性的：暂停受影响的动作，保留未受影响的工作，并且仅在充分修复后恢复。我们提出 NAQD-Env，这是一个合成环境，依据显式的证据、授权和约束依赖，将此类决策与确定性参考策略进行比较评估。十一个依赖族支持在开发结构、留出族以及留出结构组合上进行评估。指标区分尝试违规与由模拟执行门允许的违规，并联合报告策略一致性、任务价值、撤回、恢复和事件报告。我们在 350 个冻结场景上，在三种提示条件下评估来自两个家族的三个开放权重指令微调模型，在门重放之前产生 3,150 个模型-提示回合。在所报告的条件下，撤回召回率至多为 0.06，在合格机会上没有观察到有效恢复，并且只有一个回合与完整参考策略匹配。在 NAQD 提示下，Qwen2.5-7B 的不安全尝试回合少于 Qwen2.5-3B 和 Llama-3.1-8B，但完成的有用工作也更少，并且保留未受影响动作的准确性更低。探索性监督微调探针将 Qwen2.5-3B 的决策准确率从 0.45-0.54 提高到 0.83-0.92；单独诊断揭示在课程省略后出现不适当的撤回以及事件报告的丧失。这些结果促使将选择性撤回作为智能体可靠性的一个独立组成部分进行评估。该设置使用可信的结构化输入来衡量策略应用，并不确立真实世界的遏制或来源验证能力。

Original abstract: Language agents must revise planned actions when evidence changes, permission is revoked, or a stop instruction arrives. A useful response is selective: suspend affected actions, preserve unaffected work, and resume only after sufficient repair. We introduce NAQD-Env, a synthetic environment that evaluates these decisions against a deterministic reference policy over explicit evidence, authorization, and constraint dependencies. Eleven dependency families support evaluation on development structures, held-out families, and held-out combinations of structures. Metrics distinguish attempted violations from violations permitted by a simulated execution gate and jointly report policy agreement, task value, withdrawal, resumption, and event reporting. We evaluate three open-weight instruction-tuned models from two families under three prompt conditions on 350 frozen scenarios, yielding 3,150 model-prompt episodes before gate replay. Across the reported conditions, withdrawal recall is at most 0.06, no valid resumption is observed at eligible opportunities, and only one episode matches the complete reference policy. Under the NAQD prompt, Qwen2.5-7B has fewer unsafe-attempt episodes than Qwen2.5-3B and Llama-3.1-8B, but also completes less useful work and preserves unaffected actions less accurately. Exploratory supervised fine-tuning probes increase Qwen2.5-3B decision accuracy from 0.45-0.54 to 0.83-0.92; separate diagnostics reveal inappropriate withdrawal after curriculum omissions and a loss of event reporting. These results motivate evaluating selective withdrawal as a distinct component of agent reliability. The setting measures policy application with trusted structured inputs and does not establish real-world containment or source-verification ability.

</details>


### [10] [VAmoS Part Deux: Harder, More Realistic Voice-Agent Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38512)
*Joshua Meyer,Sahar Shayegan,Ritiz Tambi,Ali Khan,Sun Kim,Victor Shih,Mehdi Jamei,Andi Partovi*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 13 pages, 3 figures, 7 tables. Agent implementations: https://github.com/veris-ai/rory-agent

Selection reason: 语音 agent 基准，含多轮工具调用、验证与全通话评测，对 agent 可靠性与验证设计有参考价值，但领域偏垂直。

TL;DR: VAmoS Energy 基准通过 100 通多请求、带背景语音的公用事业账单通话评估语音代理，发现完成率仅 17.3%–44.7%，背景电视会将其大幅压低，且模拟来电者难以察觉代理的操作错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的语音代理需同时应对多请求、背景人声与不耐烦客户，但现有评估往往只关注单轮或单一维度，缺少覆盖整通电话的端到端基准。

Method: 构建 VAmoS Energy：100 通公用事业账单与付款协助通话，每通含 2–4 个请求；代理配备 16 个工具，接入有状态 Stripe 账单孪生与 Apache Fineract 贷款引擎，验证成功前禁止访问账户；采用公共家庭用电数据与宾夕法尼亚州住宅账单规则；用 LLM-as-a-verifier 对照显式要求检查代理操作与口述数字，并与代码验证器校准；在 14 个语音技术栈上每任务重复 3 次。

Result: 校准中 LLM 验证器与代码验证器在 99.1% 的检查上一致；总体完成率为 17.3%–44.7%，Grok Voice 领先，Gemini 3.8 Live 与 GPT-Live 紧随且单次通话成本相近；背景电视使汇总完成率从 38.7% 降至 8.6%；模拟来电者常因只能听到话语而无法检查操作，从而接受错误结果。

Conclusion: 语音代理评估必须覆盖整通通话，同时考察其言语内容、实际执行的操作以及应对竞争性语音的能力。

中文摘要：生产环境中的语音代理必须处理多个请求、背景语音以及失去耐心的客户。我们推出 VAmoS Energy，一个将这些挑战结合在一起的基准，包含 100 通关于公用事业账单和付款协助的通话。每位来电者提出两到四个请求。该代理拥有十六个工具，由一个有状态的 Stripe 账单孪生系统和 Apache Fineract 贷款引擎支持，并且在来电者验证成功之前账户访问被阻止。任务使用公共家庭用电数据和基于宾夕法尼亚州住宅账单规则的政策。一个 LLM 作为验证器，根据明确的要求检查代理的操作和口述数字。在一次校准运行中，它与代码验证器在 99.1% 的检查上达成一致。在十四个语音技术栈和每个任务三次重复中，完成率范围为 17.3% 到 44.7%。Grok Voice 领先，Gemini 3.8 Live 和 GPT-Live 紧随其后，每通电话成本大致相同。背景电视将汇总完成率从 38.7% 降至 8.6%。模拟来电者常常接受错误结果，因为它能听到代理的话，却无法检查其操作。这些发现表明为什么语音代理需要在整通电话中进行评估，包括它们说什么、它们改变什么，以及它们如何处理竞争性语音。

Original abstract: Voice agents in production must handle several requests, background speech, and customers who lose patience. We introduce VAmoS Energy, a benchmark that combines these challenges in 100 calls about utility billing and payment assistance. Each caller makes two to four requests. The agent has sixteen tools backed by a stateful Stripe billing twin and the Apache Fineract loan engine, with account access blocked until caller verification succeeds. The tasks use public household electricity data and a policy based on Pennsylvania's residential billing rules. An LLM-as-a-verifier checks the agent's actions and spoken figures against explicit requirements. On a calibration run, it agrees with a code verifier on 99.1% of checks. Across fourteen voice stacks and three repeats per task, completion ranges from 17.3% to 44.7%. Grok Voice leads, and Gemini 3.8 Live and GPT-Live follow at about the same cost per call. Background television reduces pooled completion from 38.7% to 8.6%. The simulated caller often accepts an incorrect result because it hears the agent's words but cannot inspect its actions. These findings show why voice agents need evaluation across the whole call, including what they say, what they change, and how they handle competing speech.

</details>


### [11] [Component-Aware Feedback for Self-Evolving Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38639)
*Ethan Lin,Jinming Nian,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 组件感知反馈与归因记忆用于 LLM 引导的程序进化，直接可迁移到自动代码优化。

TL;DR: 提出组件感知反馈，将组件编辑与指标变化归因存入记忆，并在LLM重排序多目标优化中显著提升搜索效率与质量-成本表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM引导进化搜索只保留候选程序与适应度，丢弃编辑-指标因果信息；变异LLM需从杂乱历史中猜测先前编辑效果，导致搜索慢且不稳定，尤其不利于本地LLM演化多组件系统。

Method: 比较每个评估程序与其父代，识别变化组件，记录关联指标差异到归因记忆；后续变异读取该记忆。记忆以局部（相对父代）和全局（相对种子）两个参照系保存每个变化，呈现即时效果与累积进展。在LLM重排序这一多目标、多阶段流水线质量-服务成本优化任务上验证。

Result: 在12个BRIGHT数据集上，方法在搜索预算中位数为三分之一时达到最强基线最终质量，留出nDCG@10高7.2%；成本感知目标下，找到的流水线平均更准确且每查询token少11%。

Conclusion: 组件感知反馈能提升LLM引导进化搜索的效率与稳定性，是构建更高效自演化系统的有前景方向。

中文摘要：LLM引导的进化搜索能够发现复杂程序，但现有方法大多只保存候选程序和适应度分数，却丢弃了哪些组件编辑导致了哪些适应度指标变化。现有方法迫使变异LLM从杂乱的历史中推断先前编辑的效果，使程序搜索缓慢且不稳定。对于可本地部署的LLM演化多组件系统而言尤其如此。我们引入组件感知反馈，它将每个被评估程序与其父代进行比较，识别发生变化的组件，并将其与相关指标差异一起记录到归因记忆中，供后续变异读取。该记忆以两个参照系保存每个变化：相对于其来源父代的局部参照，以及相对于种子程序的全局参照，从而既显示某个变化的即时效果，也显示自种子以来取得的累积进展。我们在LLM重排序上研究这一问题，这是一个多目标优化问题，其中多阶段流水线必须在质量与服务成本之间取得平衡。在十二个BRIGHT数据集上，我们的方法在仅消耗搜索预算中位数三分之一时，就达到最强基线的最终质量，并在留出集nDCG@10上最终高出7.2%；在成本感知目标下，它找到的流水线平均更准确，同时每个查询使用的token少11%，表明组件感知反馈是通往更高效自演化系统的一个有前景的方向。

Original abstract: LLM-guided evolutionary search can discover complex programs, but existing methods mostly only save candidate programs and fitness scores while discarding which component edits produced which fitness metric changes. Existing methods force the mutator LLM to infer the effect of prior edits from cluttered histories, making program search slow and unstable. This is especially true for locally servable LLMs to evolve multi-component systems. We introduce component-aware feedback, which compares each evaluated program with its parent, identifies the components that changed, and logs them with the associated metric differences into an attribution memory that later mutations read. The memory keeps each change in two reference frames, local against the parent it came from and global against the seed program, which shows both the immediate effect of a change and the cumulative progress made since the seed. We study this on LLM reranking, a multi-objective optimization problem where a multi-stage pipeline must balance quality against serving cost. Across twelve \textsc{Bright} datasets, our method reaches the strongest baseline's final quality after a median of one third of the search budget and ends 7.2\% higher in held-out nDCG@10, and under a cost-aware objective it finds pipelines that are on average more accurate while using 11\% fewer tokens per query, showing component-aware feedback to be a promising direction for more efficient self-evolving systems.

</details>


### [12] [ChartRevise: A Dataset and Evaluation Protocol for Exact Chart Editing via Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38642)
*Jiaxiang Tang,Yi Zhou,Chad DeLuca,Rogerio Feris,Ahmed Khalil Omran,Zhi-Li Zhang,Pengyuan Li,Ali Anwar*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 基于代码的图表编辑数据集与评估协议，涉及代码编辑精确性验证，可借鉴到 coding agent 的编辑评估。

TL;DR: 提出 ChartRevise 数据集与评估协议，用于基于程序的精确图表编辑；通过图形语法构建、源程序检查与需求校验保证编辑精确性，并采用无参考指标分别衡量原子需求完成、多余更改和遗漏耦合更新；微调后平均需求召回率和精确编辑率分别相对提升 16% 和 22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准侧重代码可执行性或图表质量，其指标无法清晰区分请求完成、遗漏的耦合更新和多余更改，因此需要面向精确程序化图表编辑的数据集与评估协议。

Method: 基于图形语法系统覆盖图表编辑操作，用源程序检查验证其在不同图表类型和库中的适用性；流程检查单项需求，对未满足者引导修复或排除；构建包含 92,438 条记录、344 种编辑类型、20 种图表类型和三个绘图库的数据集；评估采用无参考协议，分别度量原子需求完成、识别多余更改、检测遗漏耦合更新，并结合成功执行与渲染判定精确编辑成功。

Result: 数据集规模达 92,438 条记录，覆盖 344 种编辑类型、20 种图表类型和三个绘图库；在五个模型和四个外部基准上，微调使平均需求召回率相对提升 16%，平均精确编辑率相对提升 22%。

Conclusion: ChartRevise 能更精确地评估程序化图表编辑，区分需求完成、多余更改与遗漏耦合更新，并验证微调可显著提升模型编辑精确性。

中文摘要：图表编辑需要跨模态的编辑接地，即在绘制图表的代码中实现所请求的视觉更改，并进行必要的相关更新，且不改变无关内容。现有基准要么强调代码可执行性，要么强调图表质量，但其指标无法清晰地区分请求完成、遗漏的耦合更新和多余更改。我们提出 ChartRevise，一个用于精确的、基于程序的图表编辑的结构化数据集和评估协议。在数据集构建方面，我们基于图形语法来系统性地覆盖图表编辑操作，使用源程序检查来验证它们在不同图表类型和库中的适用性。为了提高编辑精确性，我们的流程检查各项需求，并在未满足时引导修复或排除。最终数据集包含 92,438 条记录，涵盖 344 种编辑类型，跨越 20 种图表类型和三个绘图库。在评估方面，我们的无参考协议分别度量原子需求完成情况、识别多余更改，并检测遗漏的耦合更新。这些检查与成功执行和渲染相结合，以判定精确编辑是否成功。在五个模型和四个外部基准上，微调在平均需求召回率上带来 16% 的相对提升，在平均精确编辑率上带来 22% 的相对提升。

Original abstract: Chart editing requires cross-modal edit grounding, realizing a requested visual change in the code that draws it, with necessary related updates and without altering unrelated content. Existing benchmarks emphasize either code executability or chart quality, but their metrics do not clearly distinguish request completion from missed coupled updates and gratuitous changes. We introduce ChartRevise, a structured dataset and evaluation protocol for exact program-grounded chart editing. For dataset construction, we build on the grammar of graphics to systematically cover chart-editing operations, using source-program checks to verify their applicability across chart types and libraries. To improve edit exactness, our pipeline checks individual requirements and guides repair or exclusion when they are unmet. The resulting dataset contains 92,438 records covering 344 edit types across 20 chart types and three plotting libraries. For evaluation, our reference-free protocol separately measures atomic requirement completion, identifies gratuitous changes, and detects missed coupled updates. These checks are combined with successful execution and rendering to determine exact-edit success. Across five models and four external benchmarks, fine-tuning yields relative gains of 16\% in mean requirement recall and 22\% in mean exact-edit rate.

</details>


### [13] [AgBench: Agentic AI Benchmarks for Personal AI Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38652)
*Yizhou Han,Di Wu,Dhananjay Saikumar,Blesson Varghese*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 15 pages, 12 figures, including supplementary material

Selection reason: 面向个人设备的 agentic AI 基准，比较本地/混合/云执行，对 agent 部署与评测有参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出AgBench，用于在个人设备上可复现评估智能体AI；基于1.6207亿数据点比较本地、混合、云端执行，发现本地执行降低任务成功率并增加完成时间但消除云API成本和敏感数据暴露，混合执行可提升成功率但云成本与数据暴露取决于任务划分与信息共享，无单一架构全面最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI系统依赖云端大语言模型带来API成本和数据暴露问题，个人AI设备支持本地执行但资源受限，现有基准难以系统评估不同设备、工作负载和部署架构间的权衡。

Method: 提出AgBench基准套件与开放工件，在个人设备上评估本地、混合和云端执行，测量任务成功率、延迟、云API成本和数据暴露。

Result: 基于超过1.6207亿个数据点：个人AI设备可本地完成许多任务，但仅本地执行通常任务成功率更低、完成时间更长，并发增加时更明显；仅本地执行消除云模型API成本和敏感信息暴露；混合执行可提升任务成功率，但云成本和数据暴露取决于工作划分与信息共享方式。

Conclusion: 没有单一架构在任务成功率、有效吞吐量、云成本和数据暴露上全面最优，部署选择应依据预期工作负载和设备能力；AgBench已开放获取。

中文摘要：智能体AI系统日益依赖云端托管的大语言模型进行规划、工具使用和迭代执行，这引发了人们对API成本和数据暴露的担忧。个人AI设备的进步使智能体能够在本地执行，但设备端资源有限可能影响任务成功率和性能。现有基准不足以系统刻画跨设备、工作负载和部署架构的这些权衡。我们提出AgBench，一个基准套件和开放工件，用于在个人设备上对智能体AI进行可复现评估。使用AgBench，我们跨各种智能体工作负载评估本地、混合和云端执行，考察任务成功率、延迟、云API成本和数据暴露。来自超过1.6207亿个数据点的结果表明，个人AI设备可以在本地完成许多智能体任务，但仅本地执行通常比仅云端执行具有更低的任务成功率和更长的完成时间，尤其是在并发增加时。仅本地执行消除了云端模型的API成本以及向云端智能体暴露敏感信息。混合执行可以提高任务成功率，但其云成本和数据暴露取决于智能体如何划分工作和共享信息。没有单一架构能在任务成功率、有效吞吐量（goodput）、云成本和数据暴露方面同时表现最佳；部署选择应反映预期的工作负载和设备能力。AgBench可在https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AgBench-2777获取。

Original abstract: Agentic AI systems increasingly rely on cloud-hosted large language models for planning, tool use, and iterative execution, raising concerns about API cost and data exposure. Advances in personal AI devices enable agents to execute locally, but limited resources on device may affect task success and performance. Existing benchmarks are inadequate for systematically characterizing these trade-offs across devices, workloads, and deployment architectures. We present AgBench, a benchmark suite and open artifacts for reproducible evaluation of agentic AI on personal devices. Using AgBench, we evaluate local, hybrid, and cloud execution across agentic workloads, examining task success, latency, cloud API cost, and data exposure. Our results, drawn from over 162.07 million data points, show that personal AI devices can complete many agent tasks locally, but local-only execution generally has lower task success and longer completion times than cloud-only execution, especially as concurrency increases. Local-only execution eliminates cloud model API costs and sensitive-information exposure to cloud agents. Hybrid execution can improve task success, but its cloud cost and data exposure depend on how agents divide work and share information. No single architecture performs best across task success, goodput, cloud cost, and data exposure; deployment choices should reflect the intended workload and device capabilities. AgBench is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AgBench-2777.

</details>


### [14] [EvoSteer: Online Self-Evolving Graph Orchestration via Reference-Anchored Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38661)
*Mingda Zhang,Hanwen Zhang,Qiang Huang,Zijia Wang,Pengfei Guo,Yuchen Zhang,Jionghao Zhu,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 在线自进化多 agent 图编排与技能准入，含信用分配和参考锚定，可迁移到 agent 自改进。

TL;DR: 提出在线自演化图编排范式EvoSteer，结合AnchorTB损失与验证式技能准入，在12个数据集四类任务上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自演化编排存在事后演化、信用扩散、技能准入未校准且从不退役等关键挑战，亟需在线修复失败步骤并可靠准入技能。

Method: 提出EvoSteer，编排器构建运行中的团队，依据执行特征与学习到的价值估计修复看似合理但失败的步骤；引入AnchorTB回归式流匹配损失，将子轨迹与冻结参考平衡从而为每个编排动作分配系数，并结合任务级参考奖励统计与前缀相关校正；提出验证式技能准入，候选技能先试用，仅当配对证据在共享名义测试预算下通过序贯检验后才晋升。

Result: 在十二个数据集上，EvoSteer在问答、数学推理、代码生成和交互式决策中显著优于基线；代码已开源。

Conclusion: EvoSteer通过在线自演化图编排、AnchorTB与验证式技能准入，有效应对现有自演化编排的关键挑战，并在多类任务和多个数据集上取得显著优于基线的表现。

中文摘要：近年来，基于LLM的多智能体系统已被广泛用于将使用工具的智能体编排为可执行的通信图。然而，现有的自演化编排仍面临关键挑战，包括：仅在轨迹结束后才修订团队的事后演化；在混杂基线（confounded baselines）下赋予每个动作相同终端优势的信用扩散（credit diffusion）；以及未经校准且从不退役的技能准入。为应对这些挑战，我们提出EvoSteer，一种在线自演化图编排（Online Self-Evolving Graph Orchestration）的新范式——编排器构建一个运行中的团队，并基于执行特征和学习到的价值估计来修复其看似合理但失败的步骤。为支持这一范式，我们引入锚定轨迹平衡（Anchored Trajectory Balance, AnchorTB），一种回归式流匹配损失，通过将子轨迹与冻结参考进行平衡，为每个编排动作分配一个系数。基于学习到的流，我们进一步提出验证式技能准入（Validated Skill Admission），其中候选技能在晋升前先进行试用，仅当配对证据在共享名义测试预算下通过序贯检验时才被晋升。此外，AnchorTB将测量到的任务级参考奖励统计与依赖于前缀的校正相结合。在十二个数据集上的实验结果表明，EvoSteer在问答、数学推理、代码生成和交互式决策中显著优于基线。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/beita6969/evosteer 获取。

Original abstract: In recent years, LLM-based multi-agent systems have been widely applied to orchestrate tool-using agents into executable communication graphs. However, existing self-evolving orchestration still faces key challenges, including post-hoc evolution that revises the team only after the trajectory ends, credit diffusion that gives every action the same terminal advantage under confounded baselines, and skill admission that is uncalibrated and never retired. To address these challenges, we propose EvoSteer, a new paradigm of Online Self-Evolving Graph Orchestration -- the orchestrator builds a running team and repairs its plausible but failing steps from execution features and a learned value estimate. To support this paradigm, we introduce Anchored Trajectory Balance (AnchorTB), a regression-style flow-matching loss that assigns each orchestration action a coefficient by balancing subtrajectories against a frozen reference. Built on the learned flow, we further propose Validated Skill Admission, in which a candidate skill is tried before promotion and promoted only if paired evidence passes a sequential test under a shared nominal testing budget. Moreover, AnchorTB combines measured task-level reference reward statistics with prefix-dependent corrections. Experimental results on twelve datasets show that EvoSteer significantly outperforms baselines across question answering, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and interactive decision making. Our code is available at https://github.com/beita6969/evosteer.

</details>


### [15] [Where Scientific Search Agents Fail: Decision-Checkpoint Auditing of Exposure and Inspection Attempts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38670)
*Hongmin Li,Wanli Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 科学搜索 agent 的决策检查点审计，区分暴露/检查/作答失败，对工具型 agent 诊断有启发。

TL;DR: 论文提出决策检查点，用过程记录区分搜索智能体是否遇到、检查目标论文；在AutoResearchBench Deep上，关键词搜索与read-first最终准确率相同，但目标暴露、检查行为和工具调用模式不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅看最终答案准确率无法判断科学搜索智能体失败于未遇到目标、未尝试检查，还是检查后才给出答案，因此需要过程级、可归因的评估方法。

Method: 引入决策检查点，在推理时记录观测和工具动作且不使用基准标签，再关联目标身份与评估者标签，从记录事件中划分结果类别。

Result: 在540个可回答的AutoResearchBench Deep问题、固定目标富集环境和五种条件下：关键词搜索准确率24.6%，原始搜索17.8%；关键词条件无目标暴露且无检查的错误更少，但目标暴露后未检查的错误更多；read-first比关键词搜索多27.4%的证据搜索调用；目标检查尝试分别为199和191个问题，二者准确率均为24.6%。

Conclusion: 检查点协议能显式揭示题目层面的行为差异，将目标暴露和检查与总体准确率、总工具使用区分开，从而更细致地诊断搜索智能体。

中文摘要：最终答案准确率并不能揭示科学搜索智能体是未能遇到目标论文、尝试检查它，还是在检查后返回了一个被接受的答案。我们引入决策检查点，在推理期间记录观测结果和工具动作，但不记录基准标签；随后将目标身份和评估者标签关联起来，以根据记录的事件分配结果类别。在一个固定的、目标富集的环境中，针对540个可回答的 AutoResearchBench Deep 问题，在五种条件下，关键词搜索达到24.6%的准确率，而原始搜索为17.8%。关键词条件下，既未暴露目标也未进行检查的错误答案更少，但在目标已暴露却未被检查之后的错误答案更多。与关键词搜索相比，read-first 的记录证据搜索调用多27.4%。目标检查尝试在 read-first 下出现在199个问题上，在关键词搜索下出现在191个问题上；两种条件的准确率均为24.6%。检查点协议使这些题目层面的差异显式化，将目标暴露和检查与总体准确率和总工具使用区分开来。

Original abstract: Final-answer accuracy does not reveal whether a scientific-search agent failed to encounter a target paper, attempt to inspect it, or return an accepted answer after inspection. We introduce decision checkpoints that record observations and tool actions without benchmark labels during inference, then join target identities and evaluator labels to assign outcome categories from recorded events. Across five conditions on 540 answerable AutoResearchBench Deep questions in a fixed, target-enriched environment, keyword search achieves 24.6\% accuracy, compared with 17.8\% for raw search. The keyword condition has fewer incorrect answers with neither target exposure nor inspection, but more incorrect answers after the target is exposed and left uninspected. Compared with keyword search, read-first has 27.4\% more recorded evidence-search calls. Target inspection attempts occur on 199 questions under read-first and 191 under keyword search; both conditions achieve 24.6\% accuracy. The checkpoint protocol makes these question-level differences explicit, distinguishing target exposure and inspection from aggregate accuracy and total tool use.

</details>


### [16] [Budget Boundary Effects in Test-Time Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38699)
*Guilin Zhang,Ziqi Tan,Wulan Guo,Kai Zhao,Hongyun Yang,Mei Luo,Qi Ning,Feng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted as a poster at the 6th Workshop on Mathematical Reasoning and AI (MATH-AI), NeurIPS 2026. 11 pages, 3 figures, 8 tables. Includes additional post-acceptance accounting and selection diagnostics

Selection reason: 测试时数学推理的预算边界与停止规则研究，可启发 agent 推理预算控制。

TL;DR: 通过 19,200 条公开轨迹的配对离线回放，研究累积 token 上限处严格停止与建议式完成的边界选择；发现建议式增益多来自替代弃答，实际成本比较与同上限比较结论不同，候选覆盖增加不保证准确率提升，预算曲线需同时报告上限、实际成本、合格候选、停止规则和选择器信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 累积 token 上限可能落在数学推导内部，测试时控制器需在严格停于上限与允许当前尝试完成之间取舍。作者希望量化这一边界选择，并厘清其对准确率、实际成本、候选覆盖和选择器行为的影响。

Method: 对 19,200 条公开轨迹进行配对离线回放，数据为 120 道 AIME、BrUMO 和 HMMT 问题及一个模型的两种归档配置；固定候选顺序与 16 次尝试上限；答案选择不接触参考答案和正确性标签；比较严格停止与建议式完成，并分析 4k、8k、32k 上限、实际成本、候选覆盖及对数概率选择器等条件下的表现。

Result: 三项发现：1）在 4k 上限下，建议式准确率增益大多以正确答案替代弃答；严格停止为仅使用已完成样本的选择器无法利用的未完成前缀付出成本。2）按实际成本的比较与同上限比较不同：low 中建议式 4k 在可比平均完成成本下准确率高于严格 8k；high 中建议式 4k 观测准确率比严格 32k 低 0.42 个百分点，并使用后者平均 token 的 59%；这些总体比较不能证明等计算量优越性或准确率等价。3）候选覆盖增加不保证答案准确率提高：对数概率选择器在覆盖上升时准确率下降，即使经过源级一致性修复仍如此；32k 下同上限多数准确率差异缩小至低于 1.3 个百分点。

Conclusion: 预算曲线应联合说明上限、实际成本、合格候选、停止规则和选择器信息；对严格停止与建议式完成的比较必须结合具体上限、实际成本、合格候选和选择器信息来解释。

中文摘要：累积 token 上限可能落在数学推导内部，迫使测试时控制器在停在上限处（严格）与允许当前尝试完成（建议式）之间做出选择。我们通过 19,200 条公开轨迹的配对离线回放来衡量这一边界选择：120 道 AIME、BrUMO 和 HMMT 问题，以及一个模型的两种归档配置。候选顺序和 16 次尝试上限固定，答案选择对参考答案和正确性标签不可见。得到三项发现。第一，在 4k 上限下，建议式带来的大多数准确率增益是用正确答案替代弃答；严格停止为未完成前缀付出代价，而仅使用已完成样本的选择器无法利用该前缀。第二，沿实际成本进行的比较不同于同上限比较：在 low 中，建议式 4k 在可比平均完成成本下比严格 8k 准确率更高；而在 high 中，其观测准确率比严格 32k 低 0.42 个百分点，同时使用了后者平均 token 的 59%。这些总体比较不能确立等计算量优越性或准确率等价性。第三，增加候选覆盖并不保证更高的答案准确率：一个对数概率选择器在覆盖率上升时准确率反而下降，即使在经过源级一致性修复之后也是如此。在 32k 下，同上限的多数准确率差异缩小到低于 1.3 个百分点。预算曲线应同时说明上限、实际成本、合格候选、停止规则和选择器信息。

Original abstract: A cumulative token cap can fall inside a mathematical derivation, forcing a test-time controller to choose between stopping at the cap (strict) and allowing the current attempt to finish (advisory). We measure this boundary choice with paired offline replays of 19,200 public traces: 120 AIME, BrUMO and HMMT problems and two archive configurations of one model. Candidate order and a 16-attempt cap are fixed, and answer selection is blind to reference answers and correctness labels. Three findings emerge. First, at the 4k cap, most advisory accuracy gains replace abstention with a correct answer; strict stopping pays for an unfinished prefix that the completed-only selector cannot use. Second, comparisons along realized cost differ from same-cap comparisons: advisory 4k in low has higher accuracy than strict 8k at comparable mean completion cost, while in high its observed accuracy is 0.42 points below strict 32k using 59% of its mean tokens. These aggregate comparisons do not establish equal-compute superiority or accuracy equivalence. Third, increased candidate coverage does not guarantee higher answer accuracy: a log-probability selector loses accuracy while coverage rises, including after a source-grade consistency repair. Same-cap majority-accuracy differences shrink below 1.3 percentage points at 32k. Budget curves should jointly state the cap, realized cost, eligible candidates, stopping rule and selector information.

</details>


### [17] [Staying on Task: Testing the Foundations of Long-Horizon Agent Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38712)
*Jeffrey Willette,Krishna C. Puvvada,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 长程 agent 可靠性诊断基准，隔离上下文长度、复杂度与格式导致的失败，直接相关 agent 长程执行。

TL;DR: 提出 Long-Transduction 受控诊断，测试模型在长生成中持续执行依赖输入上下文的读取-修改-输出操作；对七个开放权重模型评估发现，上下文长度、输入格式和局部任务复杂度分别带来 62.8%、36.5%、39.9% 的性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体工作流中，上下文增长、子任务复杂度变化和新数据到达会构成相互独立的失败轴；需要隔离这些因素来诊断模型为何在长生成中失位或不一致地执行操作。

Method: Long-Transduction 通过长生成任务要求模型持续读取、修改并输出依赖输入上下文的操作（算术、排序、变量查找、表格转换），并独立变化局部任务复杂度、输入数据格式和上下文长度，从而隔离各轴失败。评估七个开放权重模型。

Result: 在七个开放权重模型上，上下文长度从 4K 扩展到 128K 时性能下降 62.8%，改变输入格式下降 36.5%，提高局部任务复杂度下降 39.9%。

Conclusion: 这些失败表明，长时程智能体工作流在长上下文、输入格式变化和复杂度上升下存在关键能力短板与可靠性隐患。

中文摘要：长时程智能体工作流要求模型在上下文增长、子任务复杂度变化以及新数据到达的同时，持续执行重复的、依赖状态的动作。每种情况都代表一条独立的轴，智能体可能沿该轴失败。例如，一个智能体在核对长账本时，必须反复读取其状态、更新正确记录，并在数千个输出中保持对齐。模型可能接受整个账本，却在生成过程中迷失位置，或不再一致地应用该操作。我们提出 Long-Transduction，一种受控诊断方法，用于测试模型在长生成过程中保持任务执行的能力，同时持续读取、修改并输出依赖输入上下文的操作，如算术、排序、变量查找和表格转换。Long-Transduction 评估独立地改变局部任务复杂度、输入数据格式和上下文长度，以隔离每个轴上的失败。我们评估了七个开放权重模型，发现将上下文长度从 4-128K 扩展时性能下降 62.8%，改变输入格式时下降 36.5%，提高局部任务复杂度时下降 39.9%。这些失败共同构成了长时程智能体工作流中的关键隐患。

Original abstract: Long-horizon agentic workflows require models to sustain repeated state-dependent actions all while the context grows, sub-task complexity changes, and new data arrives. Each situation represents an independent axis along which an agent may fail. An agent reconciling a long ledger, for example, must repeatedly read its state, update the correct record, and preserve alignment across thousands of outputs. A model may accept the entire ledger yet lose its place or stop applying the operation consistently as generation proceeds. We introduce Long-Transduction, a controlled diagnostic that tests a model's ability to stay on task during long generation while continuously reading, mutating, and outputting input-context dependent operations such as arithmetic, sorting, variable lookups, and table transformations. Long-Transduction evaluation independently varies local task complexity, input data formatting, and context length isolate failures along each axis. We evaluate seven open-weight models, finding a 62.8\% decrease when scaling context length from 4-128K, a 36.5\% decrease when varying input format, and a 39.9\% decrease by increasing local task complexity. Together, these failures represent critical liabilities in long-horizon agentic workflows.

</details>


### [18] [Code to Control: Synthesizing Parameterized Reactive Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38733)
*Zergham Ahmed,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Chris Bates,Samuel J. Gershman*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 17 pages. Code: https://github.com/ZerghamAhmed/code-to-control

Selection reason: LLM 合成程序结构加无导数参数搜索生成控制器，兼具程序合成与搜索优化，可迁移到 code agent。

TL;DR: Code to Control通过LLM合成Python控制器结构并用无导数搜索拟合参数，学习后控制器直接作为策略执行，无需LLM推理或规划，实现实时控制，并在多类任务中优于规划式程序合成、与深度强化学习竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的控制方法要么在每次决策时调用语言模型选择动作，要么合成需要规划的世界模型，导致决策延迟较高，难以满足实时控制需求。

Method: 提出Code to Control：将控制器程序结构与参数分离。LLM负责合成控制器结构，无导数搜索利用环境反馈拟合连续控制参数。学习完成后，控制器直接作为策略执行，决策时不需要LLM推理或规划。

Result: 在Atari游戏、Flappy Bird和MuJoCo任务中，Code to Control优于基于规划的程序合成方法；以更少的环境交互与深度强化学习保持竞争力；能跨环境动力学的显著变化迁移；可扩展到复杂运动任务；在计时协议下动作选择比PPO策略更快。

Conclusion: Code to Control通过将LLM合成的程序结构学习为可直接执行的策略，消除了决策时的LLM推理与规划开销，为实时控制提供了一种高效且可迁移的途径。

中文摘要：近期基于LLM的控制方法要么调用语言模型来选择动作，要么合成需要在每一步决策时进行规划的世界模型，这会引入延迟，从而限制实时使用。我们提出Code to Control，一种合成可直接作为策略执行的Python控制器的方法。Code to Control将程序结构与参数分离。LLM合成控制器结构，而无导数搜索利用环境反馈为其参数进行拟合，以用于连续控制。一旦学习完成，所得控制器在决策时既不需要LLM推理也不需要规划，从而实现实时游戏，并且根据我们的计时协议，其动作选择比PPO策略更快。在一系列Atari游戏、Flappy Bird和MuJoCo任务上，Code to Control优于基于规划的程序合成方法，在使用更少环境交互的情况下与深度强化学习保持竞争力，能够跨环境动力学的显著变化进行迁移，并可扩展到复杂运动任务。

Original abstract: Recent LLM-based approaches to control either invoke a language model to select actions or synthesize world models that require planning at every decision, introducing latency that can limit real-time use. We introduce Code to Control, an approach that synthesizes Python controllers which execute directly as policies. Code to Control separates program structure from parameters. An LLM synthesizes the controller structure, while derivative-free search fits its parameters for continuous control using feedback from the environment. Once learned, the resulting controllers require neither LLM inference nor planning at decision time, enabling real-time gameplay and, under our timing protocol, faster action selection than a PPO policy. Across a suite of Atari games, Flappy Bird, and MuJoCo tasks, Code to Control outperforms planning-based program synthesis methods, remains competitive with deep reinforcement learning while using fewer environment interactions, transfers across substantial changes in environment dynamics, and scales to complex locomotion tasks.

</details>


### [19] [Learning to Route in Visual Space via Multi-Step Embedding Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38743)
*Tianyu Chen,Mingyuan Zhou,Jiaxing Wu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 视觉空间多步检索工具训练，提升 agent 检索与规划效率，对 tool-use agent 有参考价值。

TL;DR: 论文提出 VHOP 基准与 VHOP-Router 端到端训练方法，将多步视觉检索卸载给检索工具，在单次工具调用中于视觉潜空间完成自回归图像链检索，显著提升视觉智能体搜索性能并大幅降低 token 与 API 开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉智能体搜索受限于单步检索器，智能体需为每个中间步骤生成文本查询；当视觉线索难以描述或 top 检索结果缺少必要中间证据时性能严重受限。作者假设将整个嵌入空间上的多步导航直接交给检索工具可解决该瓶颈。

Method: 提出 VHOP 数据生成框架与基准，含五个核心难度级别，覆盖视觉匹配与搜索规划；并提出 VHOP-Router，结合监督微调、在线模仿学习与强化学习的端到端流程，将标准嵌入模型训练为自回归多步检索器，直接在视觉潜空间中进行单次工具调用的链接图像链检索。

Result: VHOP-Router 将检索性能从低于 5% 提升至 76.3%；在智能体搜索中任务成功率提高 52.7%，平均 token 长度从 1886 降至 728（减少 61%），而仅升级智能体仅增益 3.7%；相比每步检索 top 50 的强基线，保持更优性能并将上下文图像减少 23 倍、累计 API 负载削减 35 倍；对未见难度级别和真实测试集具备稳健泛化能力。

Conclusion: VHOP 与 VHOP-Router 为视觉智能体搜索提供了高效有效的解决方案，并在完全不影响原生 LLM 能力的前提下取得显著提升。

中文摘要：LLM 智能体依赖检索工具访问外部知识，但视觉智能体搜索仍受到标准单步检索器的严重瓶颈限制。在当前流程中，智能体必须为每个中间步骤发出文本查询；当视觉线索难以描述，或检索器无法在其 top 结果中呈现必要的中间证据时，就会陷入困难。我们假设，将跨整个嵌入空间的多步导航直接卸载给检索工具，可以解决这一性能瓶颈。为了系统性地研究这一点，我们引入 VHOP，一个灵活的数据生成框架与基准，包含五个核心难度级别，同时测试视觉匹配和搜索规划。利用该框架，我们开发了 VHOP-Router，一个端到端训练流程——结合监督微调、在线模仿学习和强化学习——将标准嵌入模型转化为自回归多步检索器。VHOP-Router 直接在视觉潜空间中运行，在单次工具调用中检索链接的图像链，而无需智能体制定中间文本查询。实验表明，VHOP-Router 将检索性能从低于 5% 提升到 76.3%。在智能体搜索中，它将任务成功率提高 52.7%，并将平均 token 长度从 1886 减少 61% 至 728，而升级智能体仅带来 3.7% 的增益。与智能体在每个步骤检索 top 50 结果的强基线相比，VHOP-Router 在保持更优性能的同时，将上下文中的图像减少 23 倍，并将累计 API 负载削减 35 倍。这些模型还能稳健泛化到未见过的难度级别和真实测试集。最终，VHOP 和 VHOP-Router 为视觉智能体搜索提供了一种高效且有效的解决方案，同时完全不影响原生 LLM 能力。

Original abstract: LLM agents rely on retrieval tools to access external knowledge, yet visual agentic search remains severely bottlenecked by standard single-step retrievers. In current pipelines, the agent must issue text queries for every intermediate step, struggling when visual clues are difficult to describe or when the retriever fails to surface necessary intermediate evidence within its top results. We hypothesize that offloading multi-step navigation across the entire embedding space directly to the retrieval tool resolves this performance bottleneck. To study this systematically, we introduce VHOP, a flexible data generation framework and benchmark with five core difficulty levels testing both visual matching and search planning. Using this framework, we develop VHOP-Router, an end-to-end training pipeline---combining supervised fine-tuning, online imitation learning, and reinforcement learning---that transforms a standard embedding model into an autoregressive multi-step retriever. Operating directly in the visual latent space, VHOP-Router retrieves linked image chains in a single tool call without requiring the agent to formulate intermediate text queries. Experiments show VHOP-Router boosts retrieval performance from under 5\% to 76.3\%. In agentic search, it improves task success rates by 52.7\% and reduces the average token length by 61\% from 1886 to 728, whereas upgrading the agent yields only a 3.7\% gain. Compared to a strong baseline where the agent retrieves the top 50 results per step, VHOP-Router maintains superior performance while reducing in-context images by $23\times$ and cutting the cumulative API payload by $35\times$. The models also generalize robustly to unseen difficulty levels and realistic test sets. Ultimately, VHOP and VHOP-Router provide an efficient and effective solution for visual agentic search that leaves native LLM capabilities entirely intact.

</details>


### [20] [Self-Evolving Algorithm-Design Agents: Escaping In-Context Evolutionary Stagnation via Population-Curated Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38757)
*Chen Lu,Ke Xue,Siyuan Xu,Mingxuan Yuan,Chao Qian*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 自进化算法设计 agent 与种群策展策略优化，在 GPU kernel 上获显著加速，直接相关自动代码优化。

TL;DR: 提出样本高效的参数化自进化与PCPO，使算法设计智能体通过自生成算法进行参数学习；在芯片布局学习率调度和GPU内核设计上均显著优于基线，并降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯上下文进化智能体在需要专业知识的领域易停滞，而传统参数化适配又受限于高质量领域算法稀缺，因此需要样本高效的参数化自进化方法。

Method: 刻画上下文进化停滞并提出改进链命题；基于局部迁移视角提出PCPO，利用全局种群与混合策略更新，保留并重用高质量、多样的自生成算法，将策略推向更强算法。

Result: 在全局布局学习率调度任务中仅用4个芯片案例训练，PCPO在16个芯片案例上平均超过OpenEvolve、ShinkaEvolve等最先进上下文进化方法；8B基座模型达到与GPT-5.5等前沿闭源模型相当的性能；降低推理token成本；在4个GPU内核设计上相较PyTorch Eager平均加速8.27×。

Conclusion: 参数化自进化可通过自生成算法内化领域知识，提升复杂算法设计性能并降低推理开销，具有跨任务有效性。

中文摘要：大型语言模型正越来越多地以算法设计智能体的形式参与复杂的现实世界任务，设计并改进算法。许多成功的算法设计智能体采用纯上下文进化框架，但在需要专业知识的领域，它们可能很快陷入停滞。参数化适配提供了一种内化专业知识的方式，但传统训练需要丰富的领域特定语料库，而在复杂算法设计场景中高质量算法却十分稀缺。本文提出样本高效的参数化自进化，使智能体能够探索并从自生成算法中学习。首先，我们刻画了上下文进化停滞，并分析性地提出“改进链”命题，表明学习相继的自生成算法如何能够局部提高邻近算法的似然。受这种局部迁移视角启发，我们进一步提出种群精选策略优化（PCPO），利用全局种群和混合策略更新方案来保留并重用高质量、多样的自生成算法，使策略向更强算法偏移。在电子设计自动化中全局布局的学习率调度设计任务上，仅用4个芯片案例训练，PCPO在16个芯片案例上平均优于最先进的上下文进化方法（如 OpenEvolve 和 ShinkaEvolve）。在80亿参数规模的基座模型上，PCPO取得了与 GPT-5.5 等前沿闭源模型相竞争的性能。PCPO还通过内化有依据的领域知识和提示蒸馏，降低了推理时的 token 成本。此外，PCPO在四个 GPU 内核设计上取得显著加速，相较 PyTorch Eager 基线平均加速 8.27×。

Original abstract: Large language models are increasingly participating in complex real-world tasks in the form of algorithm-design agents, designing and refining algorithms. Many successful algorithm-design agents adopt pure in-context evolutionary frameworks, but they may quickly plateau in domains that require specialized knowledge. Parametric adaptation offers a way to internalize specialized knowledge, but conventional training requires abundant domain-specific corpora while high-quality algorithms are scarce in complex algorithm-design scenarios. In this paper, we propose sample-efficient parametric self-evolution where agents can explore and learn from self-generated algorithms. First, we characterize in-context evolutionary stagnation and analytically propose the Improvement Chain proposition, showing how learning successive self-generated algorithms can locally increase the likelihood of neighboring algorithms. Motivated by this local-transfer perspective, we further propose Population-Curated Policy Optimization (PCPO) to utilize a global population and a hybrid policy update scheme for retaining and reusing high-quality, diverse self-generated algorithms, shifting the policy towards stronger algorithms. In the task of learning rate schedule design for global placement in electronic design automation, trained only on 4 chip cases, PCPO outperforms the state-of-the-art in-context evolutionary methods (e.g., OpenEvolve and ShinkaEvolve) on average across 16 chip cases. With an 8B-size base model, PCPO achieves competitive performance compared to frontier closed-source models such as GPT-5.5. PCPO also reduces inference-time token cost by internalizing grounded domain knowledge and prompt distillation. Moreover, PCPO achieves significant speedups on four GPU kernel designs, with an average of 8.27$\times$ speedup against the PyTorch Eager baseline.

</details>


### [21] [PathAnchor: Path-Structured Evidence for Scientific Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38766)
*Qiuhui Chen,Jiafan Lu,Shuaimin Tang,Tao Dai,Suyuan Wang,Chenrui Ji,Zhenglei Zhou,Weimin Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出路径结构化证据工作区与只读工具控制器，证明在固定调用预算下证据组织方式影响检索召回与引用完整性，对 agent 的记忆/证据组织设计有直接启发。

TL;DR: PathAnchor用路径结构化证据工作空间组织科学证据，通过只读工具检索并追踪“材料—传感器—信号—系统”轨迹，在柔性传感器问答中提升答案准确率、来源召回率与引用完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学智能体即使检索到相关段落，也可能丢失功能顺序、混合来源证据或给出超出证据的结论，因此需要保留证据角色、方向及转换依据的组织方式。

Method: 提出PathAnchor：将段落或抽取概念替换为源链接的“材料—传感器—信号—系统”路径记录；控制器使用三个只读工具搜索论文特定轨迹、跨候选来源追踪路径并打开确切证据，最终生成带主张引用的答案和显式证据边界。

Result: 在120个单论文和跨论文柔性传感器问题上得分82.6%，领先六个被评估系统。在匹配控制器、语料库和六次调用预算下，用路径结构化记录替换无序概念图，将来源召回率从61.3%提高到82.9%，使全部主张均引用已打开证据的答案比例从69.2%提高到90.0%，并减少工具调用。

Conclusion: 在固定智能体资源下，证据组织方式会显著影响检索效果与引用完整性。

中文摘要：科学智能体能够检索相关段落，却仍可能丢失功能顺序、混合不同来源的证据，或给出超出已检索记录的结论。我们提出 PathAnchor，一个基于路径结构化证据工作空间的有界科学推理系统。该系统不把段落或抽取概念视为独立单元，而是检索源链接的“材料—传感器—信号—系统”轨迹，这些轨迹保留角色、方向以及支持每次转换的证据。一个控制器使用三个只读工具来搜索特定论文的轨迹、跨候选来源追踪路径，并在生成带主张引用的答案和明确证据边界之前打开确切证据。在120个单论文和跨论文柔性传感器问题上，PathAnchor得分82.6%，领先于六个被评估系统。在匹配的控制器、语料库和六次调用预算下，用路径结构化记录替换无序概念图，将来源召回率从61.3%提升到82.9%，使所有主张均引用已打开证据的答案比例从69.2%提高到90.0%，并减少工具调用。这些结果表明，在固定智能体资源下，证据组织会影响检索和引用完整性。

Original abstract: Scientific agents can retrieve relevant passages yet still lose functional order, mix evidence across sources, or state conclusions that exceed the retrieved record. We introduce PathAnchor, a bounded scientific reasoning system built on path-structured evidence workspaces. Instead of treating passages or extracted concepts as independent units, the system retrieves source-linked Material-Sensor-Signal-System trajectories that preserve role, direction, and the evidence supporting each transition. A controller uses three read-only tools to search paper-specific trajectories, trace paths across candidate sources, and open exact evidence before producing a claim-cited answer and an explicit evidence boundary. On 120 single- and cross-paper flexible-sensor questions, PathAnchor scores 82.6% and leads six evaluated systems. Under a matched controller, corpus, and six-call budget, replacing unordered concept graphs with path-structured records raises source recall from 61.3% to 82.9%, increases answers whose claims all cite opened evidence from 69.2% to 90.0%, and reduces tool calls. These results show that evidence organization affects retrieval and citation completeness under fixed agent resources.

</details>


### [22] [Action Conditioned Bisimulation For GUI Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38778)
*Hongbo Zhang,Liuyang Song,Quanquan Li,Daqian Yang,Yan Wen,Zhengtao Yao*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 为 GUI agent 记忆提出动作条件双模拟合并规则，无需训练即可判定状态等价，属可迁移的 agent 记忆管理机制。

TL;DR: 提出动作条件互模拟作为网页记忆的合并规则，在冻结智能体填充的经验预测状态图上判断状态是否同一；无需观察相似性、无需训练，并替换结果价值记忆的合并规则。MiniWoB++ 上提升成功率，对照与消融无增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网页智能体需要记忆并判断页面同一性；基于观察相似性的合并会错误合并外观相似但行为不同的 GUI 页面，例如同一部件的两个标签页或同一菜单的两行对同一点击响应不同。

Method: 将合并规则定义为经验预测状态图上的动作条件互模拟；冻结智能体行动时构建该图；两个状态仅在共享动作于可供性标签下导向一致结果与后继块时合并；不依赖观察相似性，不训练；替换现有结果价值记忆的合并规则，并用闭环比较隔离该规则。

Result: 在 MiniWoB++ 上，该方法相对无记忆智能体提高成功率；采取相同探索性绕行的对照、先前的后继表示合并、以及无动作条件的同一准则均无变化。

Conclusion: 行为等价的合并准则优于观察相似性，无需训练即可改善网页智能体记忆，且增益来自动作条件。

中文摘要：一个在网页上记住自己做过什么的智能体，必须判断两个页面何时算作同一个页面。基于观察相似性构建的记忆会合并看起来相似但行为不同的页面，而图形用户界面中充斥着这类页面：同一个部件的两个标签页，或同一个菜单的两行，对同一点击会给出不同响应。我们将合并规则定义为：在一个冻结的智能体随其行动所填充的经验预测状态图上的动作条件互模拟。只有当两个状态的共享动作在某个可供性标签下导向一致的结果和后继块时，这两个状态才合并。观察相似性从不进入该规则，且不训练任何内容。它替换了现有结果价值记忆的合并规则，因此闭环比较可以将其单独隔离出来。在 MiniWoB++ 上，它比无记忆智能体提高了成功率，而采取相同探索性绕行的对照、先前的后继表示合并，以及没有动作条件的同一准则，都没有带来变化。

Original abstract: An agent that remembers what it did on a web page must decide when two pages count as the same. Memories built on observation similarity merge pages that look alike but behave differently, and GUIs are full of such pages: two tabs of one widget or two rows of one menu answer the same click differently. We define the merge rule as an action-conditioned bisimulation over the empirical predictive state graph a frozen agent fills as it acts. Two states merge only when their shared actions lead to agreeing outcomes and successor blocks under an affordance label. Observation similarity never enters the rule, and nothing is trained. It replaces the merge rule of an existing outcome-value memory, so a closed-loop comparison isolates it. On MiniWoB++ it raises success rate over a memoryless agent, while a control taking identical exploratory detours, the prior successor-representation merge, and the same criterion without action conditioning change nothing.

</details>


### [23] [GraphCert: Bootstrap Agentic Graph Reasoning with Certified Evidence Rubrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38798)
*Weiqi Jiang,Yuchen Ying,Rui Wang,Kaixuan Chen,Bingde Hu,Shunyu Liu,Yu Wang,Tongya Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Under review

Selection reason: 以可执行自认证证据评分引导 GRPO 训练图 agent，自生成监督+证据对齐奖励的机制可迁移到 agent 自训练与验证。

TL;DR: GraphCert通过认证证据评分标准自举训练紧凑图推理智能体，无需昂贵人工标注并避免敏感图数据外泄，在GRBENCH多领域优于更大LLM智能体和后训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练图智能体依赖大规模人工标注问答与推理轨迹，成本高、难扩展；使用专有LLM生成监督又可能泄露敏感图数据。

Method: 提出GraphCert：Bootstrapped Graph Quizzer在生成控制下产生图问答与证据，经执行认证和语义筛选，将接受证据规范化为认证证据评分标准；在GRPO中同时奖励Graph Solver的证据对齐与答案正确性。

Result: 在GRBENCH五个图推理领域上，GraphCert稳定优于规模大得多的LLM智能体和后训练方法；学习策略可跨异构图领域稳健迁移。

Conclusion: 可执行自认证能有效自训练紧凑图推理智能体，并获得可复用而非领域特定的图推理能力。代码将公开。

中文摘要：图智能体通过与大语言模型（LLM）和图工具的多步交互，扩展了LLM主动探索和推理知识图的能力。然而，训练具备能力的图智能体通常需要大量问答对与推理轨迹，其人工构建成本高且难以规模化。此外，使用专有LLM生成此类监督还会进一步带来将敏感图数据暴露给外部服务的风险。因此，我们提出GraphCert，在后训练阶段利用认证证据评分标准来引导智能体式图推理。具体而言，受生成控制引导的Bootstrapped Graph Quizzer生成基于图的问答对并标注支持证据，这些证据随后经过执行认证与语义整理。被接受的证据随后被规范化为认证证据评分标准，在GRPO训练期间与答案正确性一起奖励Graph Solver的证据对齐。在GRBENCH的五个图推理领域上的实验表明，GraphCert持续优于规模显著更大的LLM智能体和后训练方法。此外，我们的分析表明，所学策略能够稳健地跨异构图领域迁移，这表明GraphCert获得的是可复用的图推理能力，而非领域特定模式。这些结果确立了可执行自认证是一种有效方法，可用于自训练紧凑型图推理智能体。我们的代码将公开提供。

Original abstract: Graph agents extend large language models (LLMs) with the ability to actively explore and reason over knowledge graphs through multi-step interactions with graph tools. However, training capable graph agents typically requires large collections of question-answer pairs and reasoning trajectories, whose manual construction is costly and difficult to scale. Moreover, employing proprietary LLMs to generate such supervision further risks exposing sensitive graph data to external services. Therefore, we propose GraphCert to bootstrap agentic graph reasoning with certified evidence rubrics during post-training. Specifically, the Bootstrapped Graph Quizzer guided by generation controls produces graph-grounded QA pairs and marks supporting evidence, which undergo execution certification and semantic curation. The accepted evidence is then canonicalized into certified evidence rubrics that later reward Graph Solver evidence alignment alongside answer correctness during GRPO training. Experiments on five graph reasoning domains in GRBENCH demonstrate that GraphCert consistently outperforms substantially larger LLM agents and post-training method. Furthermore, our analysis demonstrates that the learned policy transfers robustly across heterogeneous graph domains, suggesting that GraphCert acquires reusable graph-reasoning capabilities rather than domain-specific patterns. These results establish executable self-certification as an effective approach to self-training compact graph reasoning agents. Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [24] [When Reasoning Goes Astray: Attention Dynamics of Uncontrolled Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38817)
*Yuanhe Zhang,Ziwei Wang,Jie Ren,Haoran Gao,Zhenhong Zhou,Fanyu Meng,Cong Wu,Li Sun,Sen Su*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 刻画推理模型失控循环的注意力动态并做运行时干预，对 agent 长程执行的失败检测与运行时可靠性控制有参考价值。

TL;DR: RADAR将LRM生成建模为四种状态，利用动态注意力响应实时识别推理状态；时间分析发现失控推理的注意力分布偏离正常生成且异常趋势早于重复出现；通过注意力重对齐校正偏差，可一致减少循环并基本保持良性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRM的扩展推理虽提升复杂任务性能，却可能退化为冗余验证和持续生成循环，导致推理成本上升、资源耗尽与服务退化。现有缓解方法多依赖截断长输出或检测表面重复，难以区分正常思考与失控推理，也无法解释良性推理如何退化为有害行为。

Method: 将LRM生成操作化为四种状态；提出基于动态注意力响应的推理状态分析（RADAR），实时识别当前推理状态并刻画有效反思向失控生成的发展过程；在RADAR分析指导下，将异常注意力分布重新对齐到正常请求中观察到的模式，并考察该校正对过度反思和持续循环的影响；结合时间分析检测重复前的异常趋势。

Result: 时间分析表明，失控推理以偏离正常生成的注意力分布为特征，异常趋势在重复开始前即可被检测到。通过注意力重对齐校正这些偏差，能够持续减少循环，同时基本保持良性性能。

Conclusion: RADAR为推理如何变得失控提供了机制性解释，可指导识别关键失败阶段并设计有针对性的运行时干预。

中文摘要：大型推理模型（LRMs）通过扩展推理提升了复杂任务上的性能，但同样的过程也可能退化为冗余验证和持续生成循环。这种失控推理会增加推理成本，并带来资源耗尽和服务退化的风险。然而，现有缓解方法在很大程度上只是截断长输出或对表面重复做出反应，因此无法区分正常思考与失控推理，也无法解释良性推理如何退化为有害行为。本文中，我们将LRM生成操作化为四种状态，并进一步引入基于动态注意力响应的推理状态分析（RADAR），它能够实时识别当前推理状态，并刻画有效反思如何发展为失控生成。在RADAR分析的指导下，我们进一步将异常注意力分布重新对齐到在正常请求中观察到的模式，并考察这种校正如何影响过度反思和持续循环。时间分析表明，失控推理的特征是注意力分布偏离正常生成，且异常趋势在重复开始之前就变得可检测。通过注意力重对齐校正这些偏差，能够持续减少循环，同时基本保持良性性能。总之，RADAR提供了关于推理如何变得失控的机制性解释，为识别关键失败阶段和设计有针对性的运行时干预提供了可操作指导。

Original abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) improve performance on complex tasks through extended reasoning, yet the same process can degenerate into redundant verification and persistent generation loops. Such uncontrolled reasoning increases inference cost and creates risks of resource exhaustion and service degradation. However, existing mitigations largely truncate long outputs or react to surface repetition, and thus fail to distinguish normal thinking from uncontrolled reasoning or explain how benign reasoning degenerates into harmful behavior. In this paper, we operationalize LRM generation as four states and further introduce Reasoning-state Analysis via Dynamic Attention Responses (RADAR), which identifies the current reasoning state in real time and characterizes how effective reflection can develop into uncontrolled generation. Guided by RADAR's analysis, we further realign abnormal attention distributions toward patterns observed in normal requests and examine how this correction affects excessive reflection and persistent looping. Temporal analyses show that uncontrolled reasoning is characterized by attention distributions that deviate from normal generation, with abnormal trends becoming detectable before repetition begins. Correcting these deviations through Attention Realignment consistently reduces looping while largely preserving benign performance. Together, RADAR provide a mechanistic account of how reasoning becomes uncontrolled, offering actionable guidance for identifying critical failure stages and designing targeted runtime interventions.

</details>


### [25] [When Context Changes: Understanding Update Failures in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38866)
*Junyu Guo,Yuchen Fang,Shangding Gu,Costas Spanos,James Demmel,Javad Lavaei*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出 stale binding 失败与 CICM 基准，定位注意力漂移机制并给出免训练纠正，直接关系 agent 上下文/记忆更新可靠性。

TL;DR: 论文提出受控上下文记忆基准，揭示LLM智能体存在陈旧绑定失败，归因于注意力漂移，并通过无需训练的注意力重导向干预纠正多数旧值错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着偏好、目标和事实变化，LLM 智能体需在上下文中同时保留旧版本时使用当前状态，但常错误使用旧值；本文旨在研究其发生时机与原因。

Method: 构建 CICM 基准，涵盖对话和智能体日志中的更新信息跟踪与使用；用探针检测开源模型内部更新值；在 Qwen 和 Pythia 中进行组件测试；用单层 Transformer 数学分析注意力漂移；提出逐输入调整的注意力重导向干预，无需进一步训练。

Result: 发现前沿推理模型也会无法恢复当前状态；探针表明旧值作答时更新值仍可得，失败在于选择；组件测试识别注意力漂移机制：注意力分数相似时多个旧值总注意力超过当前值；干预可在多种模型家族中纠正大多数旧值错误并保留几乎所有初始正确回答。

Conclusion: 可靠上下文管理不仅要求记住更新信息，还要求模型选择并使用当前值来指导回答。

中文摘要：随着偏好、目标和事实发生变化，LLM 智能体必须使用当前状态，而较早版本仍留在上下文中。然而，它们可能用同一变量的旧值作答，我们将这一失败称为陈旧绑定。为了研究模型何时使用过时信息以及为何如此，我们提出了受控上下文记忆（CICM），这是一个用于在对话和智能体日志中跟踪并使用更新信息的基准。我们观察到，即使前沿推理模型也可能无法恢复当前状态。我们发现，在开源模型中，当模型用旧值作答时，探针仍能恢复更新值，这表明模型未能选择仍然可用的信息。在 Qwen 和 Pythia 中的组件测试确定了这种选择失败的机制：注意力漂移，即在生成答案时注意力偏向旧值而非当前值。我们研究了一个单层 Transformer，以从数学上理解这一现象如何发生：当注意力分数相似时，多个旧值合计可能获得比当前值更多的注意力。受这一解释启发，我们在无需进一步训练的情况下将注意力重新导向当前值。当直接请求当前值时，针对每个输入调整该干预措施可在多种模型家族中纠正大多数旧值错误，同时保留几乎所有初始正确的答案。因此，可靠的上下文管理不仅仅需要记住更新信息：模型必须利用它来指导自己的答案。

Original abstract: As preferences, goals, and facts change, LLM agents must use the current state while earlier versions remain in context. Yet they can answer with an old value of the same variable, a failure that we call stale binding. To study when models use outdated information and why, we introduce Controlled In-Context Memory (CICM), a benchmark for tracking and using updated information in conversations and agent logs. We observe that even frontier reasoning models can fail to recover the current state. We find that in open-source models probes can still recover the updated value when the model answers with an old one, pointing to a failure to select information that remains available. Component tests in Qwen and Pythia identify a mechanism for this selection failure: attention drift, where attention favors old values over the current one when producing an answer. We study a one-layer transformer to mathematically understand how this phenomenon happens: when attention scores are similar, several old values can together receive more attention than the current value. Guided by this explanation, we redirect attention toward the current value without further training. When the current value is requested directly, adjusting this intervention for each input corrects most old-value errors across various model families while preserving nearly all initially correct answers. Reliable context management therefore requires more than remembering updated information: models must use it to guide their answers.

</details>


### [26] [Talk2Agent: Benchmarking Voice Interfaces for Text Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38867)
*Terumi Chiba,Guangzhi Sun,Zheqi Yuan,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向 computer-use agent 的语音接口基准与免执行任务导向评测框架，对 agent 评测方法有参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出Talk2Agent基准，评估语音接口向LLM计算机使用代理传达口述指令的效果，并给出免执行、任务条件化的评估框架；在WildClawBench上，其原生投影与下游任务完成相关，且在32小时真实人类语音上比WER/CER的皮尔逊相关提高0.246。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多使用干净书面指令，但语音交互会引入转录错误，可能改变任务关键信息；传统ASR指标无法直接衡量成功执行所需信息是否被保留，因此需要评估语音接口对任务信息的保真度。

Method: 构建Talk2Agent，基于WildClawBench和OSWorld生成人类口述任务版本，评估专用ASR模型、音频LLM、上下文偏置、基于LLM的本体修复等语音接口；并因长时程任务执行昂贵且随机，提出免执行、任务条件化框架，将原始任务评分器投影到提示可寻址意图，度量语音接口后任务相关信息的保留程度。

Result: 在WildClawBench上，Talk2Agent的免执行原生投影提供了实用且基于执行的语音接口质量度量，与下游任务完成度相关；在32小时真实人类语音上，其皮尔逊相关性比WER/CER提高0.246。

Conclusion: 语音接口评估应关注任务所需信息的保真度；Talk2Agent提供了免执行且贴近执行的评估方法，优于传统转录指标，可用于衡量语音接口对LLM计算机使用代理任务完成的影响。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）计算机使用代理通常使用干净的书面指令进行评估，尽管语音正日益成为与此类系统交互的流行接口。语音输入引入了一个额外失败点：转录错误可能在代理开始推理之前改变任务关键实体、约束或目标，而传统ASR指标并不直接衡量成功执行所需的信息是否得到保留。我们提出Talk2Agent，一个用于评估语音接口向基于LLM的计算机使用代理传达人类口述指令有效性的基准。Talk2Agent基于WildClawBench和OSWorld构建任务的人类口述版本，并评估一系列语音接口，包括专用ASR模型、具备音频能力的LLM、上下文偏置以及基于LLM的本体修复。由于反复执行长时程计算机使用任务成本高昂且具有随机性，我们进一步提出一种免执行、任务条件化的评估框架，该框架将原始任务评分器投影到提示可寻址的意图上，并度量语音接口之后有多少任务相关信息得以保留。在WildClawBench上，Talk2Agent的免执行原生投影提供了一种实用且基于执行的语音接口质量度量，与下游任务完成度相关，并在32小时真实人类语音上将皮尔逊相关性较WER/CER提高了0.246。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) computer-use agents are typically evaluated with clean written instructions, despite speech being an increasingly popular interface for interacting with such systems. Speech input introduces an additional failure point: transcription errors can alter task-critical entities, constraints, or targets before the agent begins reasoning, while conventional ASR metrics do not directly measure whether the information required for successful execution has been preserved. We introduce Talk2Agent, a benchmark for evaluating how effectively voice interfaces convey human-spoken instructions to LLM-based computer-use agents. Talk2Agent builds human-spoken versions of tasks from WildClawBench and OSWorld and evaluates a range of voice interfaces, including dedicated ASR models, audio-capable LLMs, contextual biasing, and LLM-based ontology repair. Because repeatedly executing long-horizon computer-use tasks is costly and stochastic, we further propose an execution-free, task-conditioned evaluation framework that projects the original task grader onto prompt-addressable intentions and measures how much task-relevant information is retained after the voice interface. On WildClawBench, Talk2Agent's execution-free native projection provides a practical, execution-grounded measure of voice-interface quality, correlating with downstream task completion and improving Pearson correlation by 0.246 over WER/CER on 32 hours of real human speech.

</details>


### [27] [STRATA: Self-Learning Through Role-Aligned Tiered Agents for Real-Time Strategy Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38881)
*Xinhe Tian,Xiaoyue Zhang,Ziyou Zhang,Jiacheng Li,Xiaoqiang Jin,Qianchuan Zhao,Gaochen Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 8 pages, 2 figures

Selection reason: 分层角色 agent 加赛后经验卡自学习与验证的框架，其经验提炼、复用与压缩机制可迁移到长程 agent 设计。

TL;DR: 提出STRATA，一个面向《红色警戒》的角色对齐分层RTS智能体系统，通过战略、后勤、战术与复盘智能体协作，并利用跨对局自学习生成经验卡，显著提升胜率并形成不同长期战略经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型驱动的RTS指挥决策存在推理延迟高、易错过关键战术事件、过度依赖人工经验提示词、难以从历史对局中持续学习等问题。

Method: STRATA采用角色对齐分层架构：战略智能体（SA）依据全局状态和经验卡生成高层指令，后勤智能体（LA）与战术智能体（TA）负责执行；每局后复盘智能体（RA）从游戏轨迹提取候选经验，用后续对局证据验证修订，并将跨多局有效的战略经验压缩为经验卡供SA检索。

Result: 在固定场景下，使用学习到的经验卡将观察到的胜率从30%提升至100%；针对不同打法AI对手进行顺序学习也能产生不同的长期战略经验。

Conclusion: STRATA通过角色对齐分层决策与跨对局自学习，减少了对手工经验提示的依赖，提升了RTS游戏中的战术执行与长期战略学习能力。

中文摘要：即时战略（RTS）游戏要求智能体在长时间对局中协调经济发展、生产与建设、基地防御、单位组织以及进攻时机。现有研究已将大语言模型应用于RTS游戏中的指挥决策，使智能体能够读取文本化游戏状态并生成高层计划。然而，较长的推理延迟可能导致它们错过关键战术事件。完整RTS对局的复杂性和战术多样性也使现有系统严重依赖人工编写的基于经验的提示词，从过去对局中持续学习的能力有限。我们提出STRATA，一个面向《红色警戒》的、角色对齐的分层系统，并具备跨对局自学习能力。STRATA分别将游戏内的战略、后勤和战术决策分配给战略智能体（SA）、后勤智能体（LA）和战术智能体（TA）。SA基于全局游戏状态和相关经验卡生成高层指令，而LA和TA负责后勤与战术执行。每局比赛后，复盘智能体（RA）从游戏轨迹中提取候选经验，利用后续对局中的证据对其进行验证和修订，并将多局对局中受到支持的战略经验压缩为简洁的经验卡，供SA检索。我们通过经验卡的形成、学习前后完整对局比较，以及针对不同打法AI对手的经验学习来评估STRATA。在固定场景下，使用学习到的经验卡将观察到的胜率从30%提升至100%。针对不同打法AI对手的顺序学习也会产生不同的长期战略经验。

Original abstract: Real-time strategy (RTS) games require agents to coordinate economic development, production and construction, base defense, unit organization, and attack timing over long matches. Existing studies have applied large language models to command decision-making in RTS games, enabling agents to read textual game states and generate high-level plans. However, long inference latency can cause them to miss critical tactical events. The complexity and tactical diversity of full RTS matches also leave existing systems heavily dependent on manually written experience-based prompts, with limited ability to learn continuously from past games. We present STRATA, a role-aligned hierarchical system with cross-game self-learning for Red Alert. STRATA assigns in-game strategic, logistical, and tactical decisions to a Strategic Agent (SA), Logistics Agent (LA), and Tactical Agent (TA), respectively. The SA generates high-level directives based on the global game state and relevant experience cards, while the LA and TA handle logistics and tactical execution. After each match, a Review Agent (RA) derives candidate experience from game traces, validates and revises it using evidence from subsequent matches, and compresses strategic experience supported across multiple games into concise experience cards for SA retrieval. We evaluate STRATA through the formation of experience cards, full-match comparisons before and after learning, and experience learning against AI opponents with different play styles. Under a fixed scenario, using the learned experience cards increases the observed win rate from 30% to 100%. Sequential learning against AI opponents with different play styles also produces distinct long-term strategic experience.

</details>


### [28] [Consistent Plan-Act for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38891)
*Heng-Zhuang Li,Yi-Kai Zhang,Yu Wang,Yueqing Sun,Jiayuan Zhang,Qi Gu,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 揭示 planner-actor 状态不一致并提出冲突反馈与一致性微调，直接针对长程 agent 规划-执行协调失败。

TL;DR: 论文研究规划器-执行器状态不匹配问题，提出ConPAct：通过反馈矛盾并微调一致交互，提升长时程智能体任务协调与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体任务中，规划与执行常被解耦为独立智能体，二者对任务状态的理解可能不一致，导致协调失败；需要识别并缓解这种状态不匹配。

Method: 提示规划器和执行器输出结构化状态断言，程序化比较以检测显式矛盾；分析不匹配成因；提出ConPAct，将矛盾反馈给双方形成一致状态解释，并利用筛选的一致交互数据微调；同时验证提供任务相关状态信息的作用。

Result: 发现规划器-执行器状态不匹配是系统性问题；提供任务相关状态信息可减少不匹配并提升协调与性能；ConPAct在多种环境与模型配置中提升性能，如GPT-5.6-sol/terra分别作规划器/执行器时，MiniGrid成功率由38.6%升至54.4%。

Conclusion: 状态一致性既可指导推理时校正，也可用于协调训练；缓解规划器-执行器状态不匹配能提升长时程智能体任务表现。

中文摘要：长时程智能体任务要求在动态环境中进行连续交互时具备强推理能力与高效执行能力。一种常见方法通过分离的规划器和执行器角色，将高层规划与低层执行解耦。为研究这些任务中的协调失败，我们提示两个智能体给出结构化状态断言，并以程序化方式比较它们的报告，以检测显式矛盾。我们的分析揭示了对同一任务相关状态事实的系统性分歧，我们将这一现象称为规划器-执行器状态不匹配。我们进一步发现，向智能体提供任务相关状态信息可减少不匹配，并改善协调与任务性能。基于对状态不匹配的系统性分析，我们提出一致性规划-执行（ConPAct），它将检测到的矛盾反馈给两个智能体，以形成一致的状态解释，并在精选的一致交互数据上对它们进行微调，从而实现更好的协调。ConPAct在多种环境和模型配置上提升了性能，例如，当分别使用GPT-5.6-sol/terra作为规划器和执行器时，将MiniGrid成功率从38.6%提高到54.4%，表明状态一致性可以同时指导推理时校正与协调训练。
